        WS_FLAGS.ws_eof_flag = "Y"
        check_payment_status_C1()
        if WS_FLAGS.ws_found_flag == "N":
            mark_delinquent_C1()
            assess_late_fee_C1()

def calculate_premiums_C2(insurance_master: list[Any], ws_not_eof: bool, ws_eof: bool, ins_life: bool, ins_health: bool, ins_auto: bool, ins_home: bool, ins_umbrella: bool, ins_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_life_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_health_base_premium: Decimal, ws_auto_base_premium: Decimal, ws_home_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_umbrella_rate: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate premiums."""
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        try:
            insurance_record = insurance_master.pop(0)
            determine_base_premium_C2(ins_life, ins_health, ins_auto, ins_home, ins_umbrella, ins_coverage_amount, ws_life_rate_per_1000, ws_health_base_premium, ws_auto_base_premium, ws_home_rate_per_1000, ws_umbrella_rate)
            apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_record.ins_claims_count)
            calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_record)
        except IndexError:
            ws_eof = True

def determine_base_premium_C2(ins_life: bool, ins_health: bool, ins_auto: bool, ins_home: bool, ins_umbrella: bool, ins_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_life_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_health_base_premium: Decimal, ws_auto_base_premium: Decimal, ws_home_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_umbrella_rate: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determine base premium."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if ins_life:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_life_rate_per_1000
    elif ins_health:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ws_health_base_premium
    elif ins_auto:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ws_auto_base_premium
    elif ins_home:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_home_rate_per_1000
    elif ins_umbrella:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ws_umbrella_rate

def apply_risk_factor_C2(ins_claims_count: int) -> None:
    """Apply risk factor."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')

def calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_record: Any) -> None:
    """Calculate final premium."""
    insurance_record.ins_premium_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    global WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_portfolio_value_C2(investment_master: list[Any]) -> None:
    """Calculate portfolio values."""
    ws_not_eof = True
    global WS_EOF
    while not WS_EOF:
        try:
            investment_record = investment_master.pop(0)
            calculate_position_value_C2(investment_record)
            calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_record)
            update_totals_C2(investment_record)
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF = True

def calculate_position_value_C2(investment_record: Any) -> None:
    """Calculate position value."""
    investment_record.inv_market_value = investment_record.inv_quantity * investment_record.inv_current_price

def calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_record: Any) -> None:
    """Calculate gain loss."""
    investment_record.inv_gain_loss = investment_record.inv_market_value - (investment_record.inv_quantity * investment_record.inv_purchase_price)

def update_totals_C2(investment_record: Any) -> None:
    """Update totals."""
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_record.inv_market_value

def compute_dividend_C2(investment_record: Any) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT = investment_record.inv_market_value * investment_record.inv_dividend_rate / 4

def write_transaction_C2(ws_current_timestamp: str, ws_calc_amount: Decimal, transaction_record: list[Any]) -> None:
    """Write transaction."""
    tran_timestamp = ws_current_timestamp
    tran_type = 'DEP'
    tran_amount = ws_calc_amount
    tran_status = 'C'
    transaction_record.append((tran_timestamp, tran_type, tran_amount, tran_status))

def calculate_tax_C2(ws_calc_amount: Decimal, ws_bracket_1_max: Decimal, ws_bracket_1_rate: Decimal, ws_bracket_2_max: Decimal, ws_bracket_2_rate: Decimal, ws_bracket_3_max: Decimal, ws_bracket_3_rate: Decimal, ws_bracket_5_rate: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate tax."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    if ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_1_max:
        WS_CALC_TAX = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_1_rate
    elif ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_2_max:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_1_max) * ws_bracket_2_rate)
    elif ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_3_max:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + ((ws_bracket_2_max - ws_bracket_1_max) * ws_bracket_2_rate) + ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_2_max) * ws_bracket_3_rate)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_5_rate

    formatted_count = str(ws_tran_count)
    print("TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: ", formatted_count)
    formatted_count = str(ws_loan_count)
    print("LOANS PROCESSED:        ", formatted_count)
    formatted_count = str(ws_error_count)
    print("ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     ", formatted_count)
    print("============================================")
    formatted_amount = str(ws_total_deposits)
    print("TOTAL DEPOSITS:    ", formatted_amount)
    formatted_amount = str(ws_total_withdrawals)
    print("TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: ", formatted_amount)
    formatted_amount = str(ws_total_interest)
    print("TOTAL INTEREST:    ", formatted_amount)
    formatted_amount = str(ws_total_fees)
    print("TOTAL FEES:        ", formatted_amount)
    print("============================================")

    print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    global WS_EOF
    while not WS_EOF:
        try:
            customer_record = customer_master.pop(0)

            update_customer_profile_C2(customer_record)
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF = True

def calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_record: Any) -> None:
    """Calculate risk score."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = 0
    if customer_record.cust_credit_score < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if customer_record.cust_total_loans > customer_record.cust_total_balance:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

def update_customer_profile_C2(customer_record: Any) -> None:
    """Update customer profile."""
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
        customer_record.cust_risk_rating = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        customer_record.cust_risk_rating = 'M'
    else:
        customer_record.cust_risk_rating = 'L'

def underwriting_C2(loan_payment_amount: Decimal, cust_total_balance: Decimal, loan_current_balance: Decimal, loan_collateral_value: Decimal, cust_credit_score: int) -> None:
    """Performing underwriting."""
    print("PERFORMING UNDERWRITING...")
    dti_calculation_C2(loan_payment_amount, cust_total_balance)
    ltv_calculation_C2(loan_current_balance, loan_collateral_value)
    credit_analysis_C2(cust_credit_score)

def dti_calculation_C2(loan_payment_amount: Decimal, cust_total_balance: Decimal) -> None:
    """DTI calculation."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    WS_CALC_RESULT = loan_payment_amount / (cust_total_balance / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal('0.43'):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def ltv_calculation_C2(loan_current_balance: Decimal, loan_collateral_value: Decimal) -> None:
    """LTV calculation."""
    global LOAN_LTV_RATIO, WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT, WS_CALC_FEE
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO = loan_current_balance / loan_collateral_value
    if LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal('0.80'):
        WS_CALC_FEE += WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

def credit_analysis_C2(cust_credit_score: int) -> None:
    """Credit analysis."""
    global WS_NOT_APPROVED
    if cust_credit_score < 620:
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def calculate_returns_C2(investment_record: Any) -> None:
    """Calculate returns."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    if investment_record.inv_purchase_price > 0:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (investment_record.inv_current_price - investment_record.inv_purchase_price) / investment_record.inv_purchase_price * 100

def assess_risk_C2_2(investment_record: Any) -> None:
    """Assess risk."""
    global WS_TEMP_FLAG
    if investment_record.inv_stocks:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'H'
    elif investment_record.inv_bonds:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'L'
    elif investment_record.inv_mutual_fund:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'

def tax_loss_harvesting_C2(investment_record: Any) -> None:
    """Tax loss harvesting."""
    global INV_GAIN_LOSS, WS_CALC_TAX
    if investment_record.inv_gain_loss < 0:
        WS_CALC_TAX += investment_record.inv_gain_loss

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        read_customer_master_next_C3()
        if ws_eof:
            ws_eof = True
        else:
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()

def read_customer_master_next_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_eof
    # Placeholder for reading customer data
    # In a real implementation, this would read from a file or database
    # For now, let's simulate the end of file after a few iterations.'
    global customer_counter
    customer_counter += 1
    if customer_counter > 5:  # Simulate reading 5 customers
        ws_eof = True

customer_counter = 0

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = (cust_total_balance * ws_savings_rate) + \
                      (cust_total_loans * ws_personal_rate) + \
                      (cust_total_investments * Decimal('0.01'))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_temp_code
    if ws_calc_result > 10000:
        ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM'
    elif ws_calc_result > 5000:
        ws_temp_code = 'GOLD'
    elif ws_calc_result > 1000:
        ws_temp_code = 'SILVER'
    else:
        ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_amount, acct_balance, ws_total_investments
    if acct_balance > acct_min_balance:
        ws_calc_amount = acct_balance - acct_min_balance
        acct_balance -= ws_calc_amount
        ws_total_investments += ws_calc_amount

def extract_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof, ws_process_count
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        read_customer_master_next_C3()
        if ws_eof:
            ws_eof = True
        else:
            ws_process_count += 1

def timeliness_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count
    if cust_last_activity < ws_current_date - 365:
        # Assuming ws_current_date is a date object
        ws_error_count += 1

@dataclass
class CustomerMasterC3:
    """Represents a customer master record."""
    cust_id: str = ""
    cust_name: str = ""
    cust_last_name: str = ""
    cust_state: str = ""
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_last_activity: int = 0
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

cust_master: CustomerMasterC3 = CustomerMasterC3()

# Global variables
ws_annual_fee_card: Decimal = Decimal('50.00')
ws_wire_fee_domestic: Decimal = Decimal('25.00')
ws_wire_fee_intl: Decimal = Decimal('45.00')

ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_savings_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.02')
ws_personal_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.05')

ws_not_approved: bool = False
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False

ws_temp_code: str = ""
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_error_count: int = 0
ACCT_MIN_BALANCE = 1000
ACCT_BALANCE = 5000
acct_balance: Decimal = Decimal(str(ACCT_BALANCE))
acct_min_balance: Decimal = Decimal(str(ACCT_MIN_BALANCE))

loan_delinquent: bool = True  # Example default value
cust_credit_score: int = 550     # Example default value
ws_current_date = 20240101
cust_id: str = "12345"
cust_name: str = "John"
cust_last_name: str = "Doe"
cust_state: str = "California"
cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal('10000')
cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal('5000')
cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal('2000')

@dataclass
class DataItemC4:
    """Data item representation."""
    cust_status: Optional[str] = None
    cust_ssn: Optional[str] = None
    ws_temp_code: Optional[str] = None
    ws_calc_result: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_deposits: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_calc_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_fees: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_not_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    transaction_log: Optional[str] = None
    tran_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_process_count: Optional[int] = None
    ws_error_count: Optional[int] = None
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_risk_rating: Optional[str] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_current_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
    ws_temp_string: Optional[str] = None
    ws_valid: Optional[bool] = None
    loan_current_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_paid_off: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_atm_fee_foreign: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_formatted_count: Optional[str] = None
    ws_cust_count: Optional[int] = None

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Monitoring transactions."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    data_item.ws_not_eof = True
    data_item.ws_eof = False  # Initialize ws_eof to False
    while not data_item.ws_eof:
        # Simulate READ transaction_log NEXT, AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        # For demonstration, let's assume we have a list of transaction amounts'
        transactions = [12000, 6000, 4000, 11000, 7000, 3000]  # Sample transactions
        if len(transactions) > 0:
            tran = transactions.pop(0)  # Get the next transaction
            data_item.tran_amount = Decimal(tran)  # Assign the transaction amount
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4(data_item)
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4(data_item)
            c130_network_analysis_C4(data_item)
        else:
            data_item.ws_eof = True

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    if data_item.tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4(data_item)
    if 5000 <= data_item.tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4(data_item)

def c111_flag_ctr_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    data_item.ws_process_count += 1

def c112_check_structuring_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Check structuring."""
    data_item.ws_error_count += 1

def d110_classification_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Classification."""
    if data_item.cust_credit_score > 750:
        data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif data_item.cust_credit_score > 650:
        data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif data_item.cust_credit_score > 550:
        data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Regression."""
    data_item.ws_calc_result = (data_item.cust_credit_score * 10) + \
                              (data_item.cust_total_balance / 1000) - \
                              (data_item.cust_total_loans / 2000)

def main_loop_C5() -> None:
    """Main processing loop."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF
    WS_EOF: bool = False
    while not WS_EOF:
        try:
            customer_master: Any = get_next_customer()
            update_profile_C5()
            enrich_profile_C5()
            global WS_CUST_COUNT
            WS_CUST_COUNT += 1
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF = True

def open_files_C5() -> None:
    """Open files."""
    global WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    try:
        CUSTOMER_FILE = open("customer.dat", "r")
        ACCOUNT_FILE = open("account.dat", "r")
        TRANSACTION_FILE = open("transaction.dat", "r")
        REPORT_FILE = open("report.txt", "w")
        ERROR_FILE = open("error.txt", "w")
        MASTER_FILE = open("master.dat", "r+")
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'
    except Exception as e:
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '99'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        abort_process_C5()

def initialize_tables_C5() -> None:
    """Initialize tables."""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX] = {}
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX]['RT_RATE'] = 0
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX]['RT_CODE'] = ''
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):
        BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX] = {}

def load_reference_data_C5() -> None:
    """Load reference data."""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, REFERENCE_FILE, WS_REF_RECORD, RT_CODE, RT_RATE
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        try:
            WS_REF_RECORD = read_reference_file()
            WS_REF_CODE = WS_REF_RECORD['code']
            WS_REF_RATE = WS_REF_RECORD['rate']
            RT_CODE[WS_TBL_IDX]  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_CODE
            RT_RATE[WS_TBL_IDX]  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_RATE
            WS_TBL_IDX += 1
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_reference_file() -> Any:
    """Placeholder for reading reference file data."""
    pass

def process_transactions_C5() -> None:
    """Process transactions."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_TRANS_COUNT, TRANSACTION_FILE, WS_TRANSACTION_REC
    try:
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC = read_transaction_file()
        WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
        validate_transaction_C5()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            process_by_type_C5()
        else:
            handle_error_C5()
    except StopIteration:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def read_transaction_file() -> Any:
    """Placeholder for reading transaction file data."""
    pass

def validate_transaction_C5() -> None:
    """Validate transaction."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID is None or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == '':
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return
    if not isinstance(TXN_AMOUNT, (int, float)):
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return
    if TXN_TYPE not in ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'
    validate_account_exists_C5()
    validate_business_rules_C5()

def validate_account_exists_C5() -> None:
    """Validate account exists."""
    global TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    search_account_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def validate_business_rules_C5() -> None:
    """Validate business rules."""
    global TXN_TYPE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG
    if TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        if TXN_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if TXN_AMOUNT > 1000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

def process_by_type_C5() -> None:
    """Process by type."""
    global TXN_TYPE
    if TXN_TYPE == 'D':
        process_deposit_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        process_withdrawal_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'T':
        process_transfer_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE == 'I':
        process_interest_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_C5()

    write_error_record()

        WS_ABORT_REASON = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'
        abort_process_C5()

        process_adjustment_C5()

    """Process payment."""

        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT

def process_refund_C5() -> None:

def process_adjustment_C5() -> None:
    """Process adjustment."""
    global ITEM_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, ITEM_AMOUNT, WS_ADJUSTMENT_COUNT
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ITEM_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if ITEM_AMOUNT > 0:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ITEM_AMOUNT
        update_account_C5()
        WS_ADJUSTMENT_COUNT += 1

def validate_batch_totals_C5() -> None:
    """Validate batch totals."""
    global WS_ACTUAL_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_COUNT, WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL, WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL, WS_ERROR_MSG
    if WS_ACTUAL_COUNT != WS_EXPECTED_COUNT:
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'BATCH COUNT MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_C5()
    if WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL != WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL:
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'BATCH TOTAL MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_C5()

def reject_batch_C5() -> None:
    """Reject batch."""
    global WS_CURRENT_BATCH, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT
    WS_REJECTION_RECORD = {}
    WS_REJECTION_RECORD['REJ_BATCH_ID']  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURRENT_BATCH
    WS_REJECTION_RECORD['REJ_REASON']  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_REJECTION_RECORD['REJ_DATE'] = get_current_date()
    REJECTION_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_REJECTION_RECORD
    write_rejection_record()
    WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT += 1

def write_rejection_record() -> None:
    """Placeholder for writing rejection record."""
    pass

def commit_batch_C5() -> None:
    """Commit batch."""
    global WS_BATCH_VALID, WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT
    if WS_BATCH_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT += 1
        update_batch_status_C5()

def update_batch_status_C5() -> None:
    """Update batch status."""
    global BATCH_COMMIT_DATE
    BATCH_STATUS = 'COMMITTED'
    BATCH_COMMIT_DATE = get_current_date()
    rewrite_batch_header_record()

def search_account_C5() -> None:
    """Search account."""
    global WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_SEARCH_KEY, ACCT_ID, WS_ACCOUNT_REC, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE, WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS
    WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'N'
    ACCT_ID  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_KEY
    try:
        WS_ACCOUNT_REC = read_master_file_with_key()
        WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = WS_ACCOUNT_REC['ACCT_BALANCE']
        WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE = WS_ACCOUNT_REC['ACCT_TYPE']
        WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS = WS_ACCOUNT_REC['ACCT_STATUS']
    except KeyError:
        WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'N'

def read_master_file_with_key() -> Any:
    """Placeholder for reading master file with key."""
    pass

def binary_search_C5() -> None:
    """Binary search."""
    global WS_LOW, WS_HIGH, WS_TABLE_SIZE, WS_FOUND_FLAG, TBL_KEY, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_MID, WS_FOUND_INDEX
    WS_LOW = 1
    WS_HIGH  = None  # TODO: was WS_TABLE_SIZE
    WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_LOW <= WS_HIGH:
        WS_MID = (WS_LOW + WS_HIGH) // 2
        if TBL_KEY[WS_MID] == WS_SEARCH_KEY:
            WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'Y'
            WS_FOUND_INDEX  = None  # TODO: was WS_MID
            break
        elif TBL_KEY[WS_MID] < WS_SEARCH_KEY:
            WS_LOW = WS_MID + 1
        else:
            WS_HIGH = WS_MID - 1

def hash_lookup_C5() -> None:
    """Hash lookup."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_HASH_TABLE_SIZE, HASH_KEY, HASH_VALUE, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_LOOKUP_RESULT, WS_HASH_VALUE
    WS_HASH_VALUE = (ord(WS_SEARCH_KEY[0]) * 31 + ord(WS_SEARCH_KEY[1])) % WS_HASH_TABLE_SIZE + 1
    if HASH_KEY[WS_HASH_VALUE] == WS_SEARCH_KEY:
        WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_LOOKUP_RESULT = HASH_VALUE[WS_HASH_VALUE]
    else:
        probe_hash_table_C5()

def probe_hash_table_C5() -> None:
    """Probe hash table."""
    global WS_HASH_VALUE, WS_PROBE_START, WS_HASH_TABLE_SIZE, HASH_KEY, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_LOOKUP_RESULT
    WS_PROBE_START  = None  # TODO: was WS_HASH_VALUE
    WS_HASH_VALUE += 1
    while WS_HASH_VALUE != WS_PROBE_START:
        if WS_HASH_VALUE > WS_HASH_TABLE_SIZE:
            WS_HASH_VALUE = 1
        if HASH_KEY[WS_HASH_VALUE] == WS_SEARCH_KEY:
            WS_FOUND_FLAG = 'Y'
            WS_LOOKUP_RESULT = HASH_VALUE[WS_HASH_VALUE]
            break
        if HASH_KEY[WS_HASH_VALUE] is None:
            break
        WS_HASH_VALUE += 1

def currency_conversion_C5() -> None:
    """Currency conversion."""
    get_exchange_rate_C5()
    apply_conversion_C5()
    round

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates PMI amount based on LTV ratio."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluates credit history and adjusts risk score."""
    ws_factor_1: str = ""
    ws_factor_2: str = ""
    ws_factor_3: str = ""
    
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """Calculates final risk score and category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category: str = ""

    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Determines loan approval status and approved terms."""
    ws_approval_status: str = ""
    ws_conditions: str = ""
    ws_approved_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_approved_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
    
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_risk_category: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_amount: Decimal = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate: Decimal
    
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
    else:
        ws_approved_rate = Decimal("0.00") 
    
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')
    
    return ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generates loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate: Decimal = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor: Decimal = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[int], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal]]:
    """Creates amortization schedule."""
    amort_interest: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_principal: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_balance: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_payment_num: List[int] = [0] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_payment_amt: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_escrow: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_total_pmt: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    
    ws_running_balance: Decimal = ws_loan_amount
    import datetime
    ws_payment_date = datetime.date.today()
    ws_payment_month: int = ws_payment_date.month
    ws_payment_year: int = ws_payment_date.year
    ws_amort_idx: int
    
    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_interest[ws_amort_idx] = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
        amort_principal[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest[ws_amort_idx]
        ws_running_balance -= amort_principal[ws_amort_idx]
        amort_balance[ws_amort_idx] = ws_running_balance
        amort_payment_num[ws_amort_idx] = ws_amort_idx
        amort_payment_amt[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
        
        if loan_mortgage:
            amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx] = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
            amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx] + ws_pmi_amount
        else:
            amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
        
        ws_payment_month += 1
        if ws_payment_month > 12:
            ws_payment_month = 1
            ws_payment_year += 1
        
        amort_payment_date: int = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1
        
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the payment split between interest, principal, and escrow."""
    amort_interest: Decimal = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal: Decimal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    
    if loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow: Decimal = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt: Decimal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_total_pmt: Decimal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, ws_running_balance

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Advances the payment date by one month."""
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    
    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int) -> tuple[Any, Any, str]:
    """Finalizes the loan and sets the loan status."""
    import datetime
    ws_loan_start_date = datetime.date.today()
    ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + datetime.timedelta(days=(ws_loan_term_months * 30))
    ws_loan_status: str = 'A'
    
    return ws_loan_start_date, ws_loan_end_date, ws_loan_status

def load_portfolio_C7() -> int:
    """Loads the investment portfolio from a file."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_hold_idx: int = 1
    ws_holdings_count: int = 0

    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_holding_rec = read_holdings_file_C7()  # Assuming this function reads the file
            ws_holding = ws_holding_rec
            ws_hold_idx += 1
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    
    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx -1
    return ws_holdings_count

def read_holdings_file_C7() -> Any:
    """Reads a record from the holdings file (dummy function)."""
    pass

def update_market_prices_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, hold_symbol: List[str], hold_current_price: List[Decimal]) -> List[Decimal]:
    """Updates the market prices for each holding in the portfolio."""
    ws_quote_price: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        ws_quote_symbol: str = hold_symbol[ws_hold_idx-1]
        ws_quote_price = get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol)
        hold_current_price[ws_hold_idx-1] = ws_quote_price
        
    return hold_current_price

def get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """Gets the current market quote for a given symbol."""
    quote_request_symbol: str = ws_quote_symbol
    quote_response = getquote_C7(quote_request_symbol)  # Assuming this function calls the 'GETQUOTE' program
    
    if quote_response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
        ws_quote_price: Decimal = quote_response.quote_last_price
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal('0.00')
    
    return ws_quote_price

@dataclass
class QuoteResponse:
    """Mock Quote Response"""
    quote_response_status: str
    quote_last_price: Decimal

def getquote_C7(quote_request_symbol: str) -> QuoteResponse:
    """Mock GETQUOTE call"""
    # Simulate an external call to 'GETQUOTE' using the quote_request_symbol
    # This is a placeholder and would need to be replaced with actual code
    # to interface with a real-time market data provider
    if quote_request_symbol == "AAPL":
      return QuoteResponse(quote_response_status="OK", quote_last_price=Decimal("170.34"))
    elif quote_request_symbol == "GOOG":
      return QuoteResponse(quote_response_status="OK", quote_last_price=Decimal("2700.50"))
    else:
      return QuoteResponse(quote_response_status="ERROR", quote_last_price=Decimal("0.00"))

def calculate_values_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_cost_per_share: List[Decimal]) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal]]:
    """Calculates the market value, cost basis, and unrealized gain for the portfolio."""
    ws_total_value: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_cost_basis: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    hold_market_value: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_holdings_count + 1)
    hold_gain_loss: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_holdings_count + 1)
    hold_pct_change: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_holdings_count + 1)
    
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_market_value[ws_hold_idx - 1], hold_gain_loss[ws_hold_idx - 1], hold_pct_change[ws_hold_idx - 1] = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx, hold_shares, hold_current_price, hold_cost_per_share)
        ws_total_value += hold_market_value[ws_hold_idx - 1]
        ws_cost_basis += hold_shares[ws_hold_idx-1] * hold_cost_per_share[ws_hold_idx-1]
        ws_unrealized_gain += hold_gain_loss[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    
    return ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain, hold_market_value, hold_gain_loss, hold_pct_change

def calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx: int, hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_cost_per_share: List[Decimal]) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the market value, gain/loss, and percentage change for a single holding."""
    ws_hold_idx -= 1
    hold_market_value: Decimal = hold_shares[ws_hold_idx] * hold_current_price[ws_hold_idx]
    ws_hold_cost: Decimal = hold_shares[ws_hold_idx] * hold_cost_per_share[ws_hold_idx]
    hold_gain_loss: Decimal = hold_market_value - ws_hold_cost
    
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change: Decimal = (hold_gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change = Decimal('0.00')
    
    return hold_market_value, hold_gain_loss, hold_pct_change

def rebalance_check_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal, ws_stocks_value: Decimal, ws_bonds_value: Decimal, ws_cash_value: Decimal) -> None:
    """Checks if the portfolio needs to be rebalanced."""
    ws_rebalance_needed: str
    ws_stocks_pct: Decimal
    ws_bonds_pct: Decimal
    ws_cash_pct: Decimal
    
    ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct = calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_total_value, ws_stocks_value, ws_bonds_value, ws_cash_value)
    rebalance_needed_flag: str = compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_target_stocks_pct, ws_target_bonds_pct)
    
    if rebalance_needed_flag == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value, ws_stocks_pct, ws_target_stocks_pct)
    pass

def calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_stocks_value: Decimal, ws_bonds_value: Decimal, ws_cash_value: Decimal) -> tuple[str, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the current allocation of stocks, bonds, and cash in the portfolio."""
    
    ws_stocks_pct: Decimal = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct: Decimal = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct: Decimal = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_rebalance_needed: str = "N"

    return ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct

def compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_bonds_pct: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> str:
    """Compares the current allocation to the target allocation and sets the rebalance needed flag."""
    ws_rebalance_needed: str = 'N'
    ws_stocks_diff: Decimal = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    ws_bonds_diff: Decimal = ws_bonds_pct - ws_target_bonds_pct
    
    if abs(ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    
    if abs(ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    
    return ws_rebalance_needed

def generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Generates the trades needed to rebalance the portfolio."""
    ws_stocks_diff: Decimal = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    ws_sell_amount: Decimal
    ws_buy_amount: Decimal
    
    if ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        ws_sell_amount = ws_total_value * ws_stocks_diff / 100
        create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount)
    else:
        ws_buy_amount = ws_total_value * (0 - ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount)
    pass

def create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount: Decimal) -> None:

def validate_order_C7(ws_trade_symbol: str, ws_trade_shares: int, ws_limit_price: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Validates a trade order."""
    ws_order_valid: str = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason: str = ''
    order_limit: bool = False #placeholder, can't see this def from COBOL'
    order_stop_limit: bool = False #placeholder, can't see this def from COBOL'
    
    if not ws_trade_symbol:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason
    
    if ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason
    
    if order_limit or order_stop_limit:
        if ws_limit_price <= 0:
            ws_order_valid = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'
        return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason
    
    return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason

def check_funds_shares_C7(trade_buy: bool, trade_sell: bool, ws_trade_shares: int, ws_estimated_price: Decimal, ws_available_cash: Decimal, ws_trade_symbol: str, ws_holdings_count: int, hold_symbol: List[str], hold_shares: List[int]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Checks if there are sufficient funds or shares for a trade."""
    ws_sufficient_flag: str = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason: str = ''
    ws_required_funds: Decimal
    ws_current_shares: int = 0
    
    if trade_buy:
        ws_required_funds = ws_trade_shares * ws_estimated_price
        if ws_required_funds > ws_available_cash:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    
    if trade_sell:
        ws_current_shares = check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol, ws_holdings_count, hold_symbol, hold_shares)
        if ws_current_shares < ws_trade_shares:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'
    
    return ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason

def check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol: str, ws_holdings_count: int, hold_symbol: List[str], hold_shares: List[int]) -> int:
    """Checks the current share position for a given symbol."""
    ws_current_shares: int = 0
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        if hold_symbol[ws_hold_idx - 1] == ws_trade_symbol:
            ws_current_shares += hold_shares[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    
    return ws_current_shares

def route_order_C7(ws_trade_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[str, Any]:
    """Routes a trade order based on the trade amount."""
    ws_routing_type: str
    
    if ws_trade_amount > 100000:
        ws_routing_type = 'ALGO'
    elif ws_trade_amount > 10000:
        ws_routing_type = 'SMART'
    else:
        ws_routing_type = 'DIRECT'
    
    import datetime
    ws_order_time = datetime.date.today()
    
    return ws_routing_type, ws_order_time

def execute_order_C7(order_market: bool, order_limit: bool, order_stop: bool, order_stop_limit: bool, trade_buy: bool, trade_sell: bool, ws_current_market_price: Decimal, ws_limit_price: Decimal, ws_stop_price: Decimal) -> str:
    """Executes a trade order based on the order type."""
    if order_market:
        ws_trade_status: str = market_order_C7(ws_current_market_price)
    elif order_limit:
        ws_trade_status: str = limit_order_C7(trade_buy, ws_current_market_price, ws_limit_price)
    elif order_stop:
        ws_trade_status: str = stop_order_C7(trade_sell, ws_current_market_price, ws_stop_price)
    else:
        ws_trade_status: str = stop_limit_order_C7(ws_current_market_price, ws_stop_price, trade_buy, ws_limit_price)
    
    return ws_trade_status

def market_order_C7(ws_current_market_price: Decimal) -> str:
    """Executes a market order."""
    ws_executed_price: Decimal = ws_current_market_price
    ws_trade_status: str = 'FILLED'
    import datetime
    ws_execution_time = datetime.date.today()
    return ws_trade_status

def limit_order_C7(trade_buy: bool, ws_current_market_price: Decimal, ws_limit_price: Decimal) -> str:
    """Executes a limit order."""
    ws_executed_price: Decimal
    
    if trade_buy:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status: str = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    else:
        if ws_current_market_price >= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    return ws_trade_status

def stop_order_C7(trade_sell: bool, ws_current_market_price: Decimal, ws_stop_price: Decimal):
    pass
def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates auto premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12
    pass

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates home premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal('200')
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12
    pass

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_insured_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates health premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal('300')
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12
    pass

def underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """Performs underwriting."""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    check_medical_history_C8()
    verify_information_C8()
    determine_decision_C8()
    pass

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, ws_risk_points: int, policy_auto: bool, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int) -> None:
    """Evaluates risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if policy_auto:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15
    pass

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_condition_points: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int) -> None:
    """Checks medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5
    pass

def verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """Verifies information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    validate_documents_C8()
    pass

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str, ws_address_mismatch: str) -> None:
    """Checks fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    pass

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validates documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'
    pass

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    pass

    pass

def set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_policy_number: str, benef_name: list[str], benef_relation: list[str], benef_pct: list[Decimal]) -> None:
    """Sets beneficiaries."""
    for ws_benef_idx in range(5):
        if benef_name[ws_benef_idx] != "SPACES": #replace SPACES with python equivalent for empty string
            # INITIALIZE ws_beneficiary_rec - Assuming a dataclass exists
            benef_rec_policy = ws_policy_number
            benef_rec_name = benef_name[ws_benef_idx]
            benef_rec_relation = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx]
            benef_rec_pct = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx]
            # WRITE beneficiary_record FROM ws_beneficiary_rec - Implement writing to file
            pass
    pass

    check_deductible_C8()
    pass

def check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks policy status."""
    if ws_policy_status != 'A':
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'
    pass

def check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks coverage."""
    if ws_claim_type != ws_covered_perils:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'
    pass

def check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks deductible."""
    if ws_claim_amount <= ws_deductible:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'
    pass

def investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Investigates claim."""
    if ws_claim_amount > 10000:
        ws_claim_status = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8()
    fraud_check_C8(ws_coverage_amount, ws_claim_amount, ws_recent_claims, ws_fraud_review)
    pass

def assign_adjuster_C8(ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str) -> None:
    """Assigns adjuster."""
    ws_adjuster_id = 'ADJ001'
    ws_notes = 'Assigned for investigation'
    pass

def fraud_check_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Checks for fraud."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 2:
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    if ws_claim_amount > ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.8'):
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    pass

def adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Adjudicates claim."""
    if ws_claim_status != 'DENIED':
        ws_approved_amount = ws_claim_amount - ws_deductible
        if ws_approved_amount > ws_coverage_amount:
            ws_approved_amount = ws_coverage_amount
        ws_claim_status = 'APPROVED'
    pass

        update_claim_record_C8()
    pass

def load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id: str, emp_search_key: str, ws_error_msg: str) -> None:
        """TODO"""
    if ws_pay_type == 'SALARY':
        calc_salary_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'HOURLY':
        calc_hourly_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'COMMISSION':
        calc_commission_pay_C8()
    pass

def calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates salary pay."""
    ws_gross_pay = ws_annual_salary / ws_pay_periods
    pass

def calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates hourly pay."""
    if ws_hours_worked <= 40:
        ws_regular_pay = ws_hours_worked * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_overtime_pay = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_regular_pay = 40 * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_ot_hours = ws_hours_worked - 40
        ws_overtime_pay = ws_ot_hours * ws_hourly_rate * Decimal('1.5')
    ws_gross_pay = ws_regular_pay + ws_overtime_pay
    pass

def calc_commission_pay_C8(ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_commission_rate: Decimal, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates commission pay."""
    ws_base_pay = ws_base_salary / ws_pay_periods
    ws_commission_pay = ws_sales_amount * ws_commission_rate
    ws_gross_pay = ws_base_pay + ws_commission_pay
    pass

def calculate_taxes_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates taxes."""
    calc_federal_tax_C8()
    calc_state_tax_C8()
    calc_local_tax_C8()
    calc_fica_C8()
    pass

def calc_federal_tax_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_exemptions: int, ws_annualized_gross: Decimal, ws_allowance_amount: Decimal, ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates federal tax."""
    ws_annualized_gross = ws_gross_pay * ws_pay_periods
    ws_allowance_amount = ws_exemptions * 4300
    ws_taxable_income = ws_annualized_gross - ws_allowance_amount
    if ws_taxable_income < 0:
        ws_taxable_income = Decimal('0')
    apply_tax_brackets_C8()
    ws_federal_tax = ws_annual_tax / ws_pay_periods
    pass

def apply_tax_brackets_C8(status_single: bool, status_married_joint: bool, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Applies tax brackets."""
    ws_annual_tax = Decimal('0')
    if status_single:
        single_brackets_C8()
    elif status_married_joint:
        married_brackets_C8()
    pass

def single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates single brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 10275:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 41775:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('1027.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 10275) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 89075:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('4807.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 41775) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 170050:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('15213.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 89075) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 215950:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('34647.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 170050) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 539900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('49335.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 215950) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('162718.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 539900) * Decimal('0.37')
    pass

def married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates married brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 20550:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 83550:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('2055.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 20550) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 178150:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('9615.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 83550) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 340100:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('30427.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 178150) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 431900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('69295.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 340100) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 647850:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('98671.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 431900) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('174253.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 647850) * Decimal('0.37')
    pass

def calc_state_tax_C8(ws_state_code: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates state tax."""
    if ws_state_code == 'CA':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0725')
    elif ws_state_code == 'NY':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0685')
    elif ws_state_code == 'TX':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    elif ws_state_code == 'FL':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.05')
    pass

def calc_local_tax_C8(ws_local_tax_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates local tax."""
    if ws_local_tax_rate > 0:
        ws_local_tax = ws_gross_pay * ws_local_tax_rate
    else:
        ws_local_tax = Decimal('0')
    pass

def calc_fica_C8(ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_remaining_cap: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_additional_medicare: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates FICA."""
    if ws_ytd_gross < 160200:
        ws_remaining_cap = 160200 - ws_ytd_gross
        if ws_gross_pay <= ws_remaining_cap:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.062')
        else:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_remaining_cap * Decimal('0.062')
    else:
        ws_fica_ss = Decimal('0')
    ws_fica_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0145')
    if ws_ytd_gross > 200000:
        ws_additional_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.009')
        ws_fica_medicare += ws_additional_medicare
    pass

def calculate_deductions_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates deductions."""
    calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8()
    calc_post_tax_deductions_C8()
    pass

def calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8(ws_401k_pct: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal, ws_health_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_hsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates pre-tax deductions."""
    if ws_401k_pct > 0:
        ws_401k_contrib = ws_gross_pay * ws_401k_pct / 100
        if ws_ytd_401k + ws_401k_contrib > 22500:
            ws_401k_contrib = 22500 - ws_ytd_401k
            if ws_401k_contrib < 0:
                ws_401k_contrib = Decimal('0')
    ws_health_ins = ws_health_ins_deduct
    ws_dental_ins = ws_dental_ins_deduct
    ws_vision_ins = ws_vision_ins_deduct
    ws_hsa_contrib = ws_hsa_deduct
    ws_fsa_contrib = ws_fsa_deduct
    pass

def calc_post_tax_deductions_C8(ws_life_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_deduct: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues_amt: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment_amt: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates post-tax deductions."""
    ws_life_ins = ws_life_ins_deduct
    ws_disability_ins = ws_disability_deduct
    ws_union_dues = ws_union_dues_amt
    ws_garnishment = ws_garnishment_amt
    pass

def calculate_net_pay_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal, ws_other_deduct: Decimal, ws_total_deductions: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates net pay."""
    ws_total_deductions = (ws_federal_tax + ws_state_tax + ws_local_tax + 0  # TODO
                            ws_fica_ss + ws_fica_medicare + 0  # TODO
                            ws_health_ins + ws_dental_ins + ws_vision_ins + 0  # TODO
                            ws_401k_contrib + ws_hsa_contrib + ws_fsa_contrib + 0  # TODO
                            ws_life_ins + ws_disability_ins + 0  # TODO
                            ws_union_dues + ws_garnishment + ws_other_deduct)
    ws_net_pay = ws_gross_pay - ws_total_deductions
    update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay, ws_federal_tax, ws_state_tax, ws_fica_ss, ws_fica_medicare, ws_net_pay, ws_401k_contrib)
    pass

def update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_ytd_fed_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_state_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_fica: Decimal, ws_ytd_net: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal) -> None:
    """Updates YTD totals."""
    ws_ytd_gross += ws_gross_pay
    ws_ytd_fed_tax += ws_federal_tax
    ws_ytd_state_tax += ws_state_tax
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_ss
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_medicare
    ws_ytd_net += ws_net_pay
    ws_ytd_401k += ws_401k_contrib
    pass

    stub_net = ws_net_pay
    stub_ytd_gross = ws_ytd_

    ws_customer_name: str = ""
    ws_watchlist_hits: int = 0

    media_search_name = ws_customer_name
    mediasrch(media_request, media_response)
    if media_hits_found > 0:
        ws_watchlist_hits += media_hits_found

def calculate_match_score_C9() -> None:
    """Calculates match score."""
    ws_ofac_score: int = 0
    ws_pep_score: int = 0
    ws_match_score: int = 0
    ws_watchlist_hits: int = 0

    if ws_ofac_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    if ws_pep_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    ws_match_score = ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits
    pass

def determine_disposition_C9() -> None:
    """Determines disposition."""
    ws_match_score: int = 0
    ws_match_type: str = ""
    ws_sar_required: str = ""
    ws_case_status: str = ""

    if ws_match_score >= 90:
        ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
        ws_sar_required = 'Y'
    elif ws_match_score >= 75:
        ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
        ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
    elif ws_match_score >= 50:
        ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    else:
        ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    pass

    pass

def check_previous_cases_C9() -> None:
    """Checks previous cases."""
    case_search_key: str = ""
    ws_customer_id: str = ""
    ws_eof_flag: str = ""
    ws_previous_case: str = ""
    ws_previous_case_count: int = 0

    case_search_key = ws_customer_id
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_previous_case = read_case_file(case_search_key) # Assuming READ case_file is read_case_file()
            ws_previous_case_count += 1
        except:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def review_notes_C9() -> None:
    """Reviews notes."""
    ws_previous_case_count: int = 0
    ws_caller_type: str = ""

    if ws_previous_case_count > 0:
        ws_caller_type = 'REPEAT CALLER'
    else:
        ws_caller_type = 'FIRST CONTACT'
    pass

def determine_resolution_C9() -> None:

    if ws_case_type == 'BILLING INQUIRY':

    elif ws_case_type == 'FRAUD REPORT':
        resolve_fraud_C9()
    elif ws_case_type == 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
        resolve_access_C9()
    else:
        resolve_general_C9()
    pass

def generate_doc_id_C9() -> None:
    """Generates doc ID."""
    ws_date_part: str = ""
    ws_random_part: int = 0
    ws_doc_id: str = ""

    ws_date_part = current_date()  # Assuming FUNCTION current_date is current_date()
    ws_random_part = random() * 999999  # Assuming FUNCTION RANDOM is random()
    ws_doc_id = 'DOC' + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)
    pass

def classify_document_C9() -> None:
    """Classifies document."""
    ws_doc_content_type: str = ""
    ws_doc_classification: str = ""

    if ws_doc_content_type == 'STATEMENT':
        ws_doc_classification = 'account_docs'
    elif ws_doc_content_type == 'tax_form':
        ws_doc_classification = 'tax_docs'
    elif ws_doc_content_type == 'CONTRACT':
        ws_doc_classification = 'legal_docs'
    elif ws_doc_content_type == 'id_document':
        ws_doc_classification = 'kyc_docs'
    else:
        ws_doc_classification = 'general_docs'
    pass

def extract_data_C9() -> None:
    """Extracts data."""
    ws_doc_type: str = ""
    ws_doc_id: str = ""
    ws_extracted_data: str = ""

    if ws_doc_type == 'PDF':
        pdfextract(ws_doc_id, ws_extracted_data)
    elif ws_doc_type == 'IMAGE':
        ocrextract(ws_doc_id, ws_extracted_data)
    pass

def store_document_C9() -> None:
    """Stores document."""
    ws_storage_request: Any = None
    ws_doc_id: str = ""
    store_doc_id: str = ""
    ws_doc_classification: str = ""
    store_bucket: str = ""
    ws_doc_size_kb: int = 0
    store_size: int = 0
    ws_storage_response: Any = None
    store_status: str = ""
    ws_doc_status: str = ""
    store_checksum: str = ""
    ws_doc_checksum: str = ""

    ws_storage_request = None # Assuming INITIALIZE means setting to None
    store_doc_id = ws_doc_id
    store_bucket = ws_doc_classification
    store_size = ws_doc_size_kb
    docstorage(ws_storage_request, ws_storage_response)
    if store_status == 'SUCCESS':
        ws_doc_status = 'STORED'
        ws_doc_checksum = store_checksum
    else:
        ws_doc_status = 'FAILED'
    pass

def apply_retention_C9() -> None:
    """Applies retention."""
    ws_doc_classification: str = ""
    ws_retention_years: int = 0
    ws_doc_created_date: int = 0
    ws_doc_retention_date: int = 0

    if ws_doc_classification == 'tax_docs':
        ws_retention_years = 7
    elif ws_doc_classification == 'legal_docs':
        ws_retention_years = 10
    elif ws_doc_classification == 'kyc_docs':
        ws_retention_years = 5
    else:
        ws_retention_years = 3
    ws_doc_retention_date = ws_doc_created_date + (ws_retention_years * 10000)
    pass

    complete_workflow_C9()
    pass
