    """Initializes workflow."""
    generate_workflow_id_C9()
    ws_workflow_status: str = ""
    ws_current_step: int = 0
    ws_workflow_start: str = ""

    ws_workflow_status = 'INITIATED'
    ws_current_step = 1
    ws_workflow_start = current_date()  # Assuming FUNCTION current_date is current_date()
    pass

def generate_workflow_id_C9() -> None:
    """Generates workflow ID."""
    ws_date_part: str = ""
    ws_random_part: int = 0
    ws_workflow_id: str = ""

    ws_date_part = current_date()  # Assuming FUNCTION current_date is current_date()
    ws_random_part = random() * 99999  # Assuming FUNCTION RANDOM is random()
    ws_workflow_id = 'WF' + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)
    pass

def execute_steps_C9() -> None:
    """Executes steps."""
    ws_current_step: int = 0
    ws_total_steps: int = 0
    ws_workflow_status: str = ""

    while not (ws_current_step > ws_total_steps or ws_workflow_status == 'FAILED'):
        execute_current_step_C9()
        ws_current_step += 1
    pass

def execute_current_step_C9() -> None:
    """Executes current step."""
    ws_current_step: int = 0
    step_start_date: list[str] = []
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_name: list[str] = []
    step_end_date: list[str] = []

    step_start_date.append(current_date())  # Assuming FUNCTION current_date is current_date()
    step_status.append('in_progress')
    if step_name[ws_current_step - 1] == 'VALIDATION':
        validation_step_C9()
    elif step_name[ws_current_step - 1] == 'APPROVAL':
        approval_step_C9()
    elif step_name[ws_current_step - 1] == 'PROCESSING':
        processing_step_C9()
    elif step_name[ws_current_step - 1] == 'NOTIFICATION':
        notification_step_C9()
    else:
        generic_step_C9()
    step_end_date.append(current_date())  # Assuming FUNCTION current_date is current_date()
    pass

def validation_step_C9() -> None:
    """Validation step."""
    ws_validation_passed: str = ""
    ws_current_step: int = 0
    step_status: list[str] = []
    step_outcome: list[str] = []
    ws_workflow_status: str = ""

    if ws_validation_passed == 'Y':
        step_status.append('COMPLETED')
        step_outcome
def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: Any, ws_next_run_date: Any, schedule_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculate the next run date based on the schedule type."""
    if schedule_type == 'DAILY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 1
    elif schedule_type == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 7
    elif schedule_type == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 30
    elif schedule_type == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 90
    elif schedule_type == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 365
    else:
        pass

def data_analytics_C10() -> None:
    """Execute data analytics and reporting procedures."""
    collect_metrics_C10()
    aggregate_data_C10()
    calculate_kpi_C10()
    generate_dashboard_C10()
    export_data_C10()

def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collect various metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10(transaction_file: Any, ws_trans_rec: Any, trans_amount: Any, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_total_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_total_trans_count: int, ws_avg_trans_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Collect transaction-related metrics."""
    ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    ws_total_trans_count = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = transaction_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_trans_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                ws_total_trans_count += 1
                ws_total_trans_amount += trans_amount
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics_C10(customer_file: Any, ws_cust_rec: Any, cust_status: str, cust_open_date: Any, cust_close_date: Any, ws_period_start: Any, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_active_customers: int, ws_new_customers: int, ws_churned_customers: int) -> None:
    """Collect customer-related metrics."""
    ws_active_customers = 0
    ws_new_customers = 0
    ws_churned_customers = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = customer_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_cust_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                if cust_status == 'A':
                    ws_active_customers += 1
                if cust_open_date >= ws_period_start:
                    ws_new_customers += 1
                if cust_close_date >= ws_period_start:
                    ws_churned_customers += 1
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics_C10(perf_log_file: Any, ws_perf_rec: Any, perf_response_time: Any, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_response_time_total: Decimal, ws_response_count: int, ws_avg_response_time: Decimal) -> None:
    """Collect performance-related metrics."""
    ws_response_time_total = Decimal('0')
    ws_response_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = perf_log_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_perf_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                ws_response_time_total += perf_response_time
                ws_response_count += 1
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_response_count > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def aggregate_data_C10() -> None:
    """Aggregate collected data."""
    daily_aggregation_C10()
    weekly_aggregation_C10()
    monthly_aggregation_C10()

def daily_aggregation_C10(ws_daily_summary: Any, ws_process_date: Any, daily_date: Any, ws_total_trans_count: Any, daily_trans_count: Any, ws_total_trans_amount: Any, daily_trans_amount: Any, ws_total_deposits: Any, daily_deposits: Any, ws_total_withdrawals: Any, daily_withdrawals: Any, daily_summary_record: Any) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_daily_summary = {}
    daily_date = ws_process_date
    daily_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    daily_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount
    daily_deposits = ws_total_deposits
    daily_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals
    daily_summary_record = ws_daily_summary

def weekly_aggregation_C10(ws_day_of_week: int, ws_weekly_summary: Any, ws_week_number: Any, weekly_week: Any, weekly_summary_record: Any) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary = {}
        weekly_week = ws_week_number
        sum_week_data_C10()
        weekly_summary_record = ws_weekly_summary

def sum_week_data_C10(daily_trans_count: Any, weekly_trans_count: Decimal, daily_trans_amount: Any, weekly_trans_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Sum data for the week."""
    weekly_trans_count = Decimal('0')
    weekly_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    for _ in range(7):
        weekly_trans_count += daily_trans_count
        weekly_trans_amount += daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10(ws_end_of_month: str, ws_monthly_summary: Any, ws_curr_month: Any, monthly_month: Any, ws_curr_year: Any, monthly_year: Any, monthly_summary_record: Any) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary = {}
        monthly_month = ws_curr_month
        monthly_year = ws_curr_year
        sum_month_data_C10()
        monthly_summary_record = ws_monthly_summary

def sum_month_data_C10(daily_summary_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, daily_month: Any, ws_curr_month: Any, ws_eof_flag: str, monthly_trans_count: Decimal, daily_trans_count: Any, monthly_trans_amount: Decimal, daily_trans_amount: Any, monthly_new_accounts: int, monthly_closed_accounts: int) -> None:
    """Sum data for the month."""
    monthly_trans_count = Decimal('0')
    monthly_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    monthly_new_accounts = 0
    monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                if daily_month == ws_curr_month:
                    monthly_trans_count += daily_trans_count
                    monthly_trans_amount += daily_trans_amount
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculate Key Performance Indicators (KPIs)."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10(ws_total_assets: Decimal, ws_net_income: Decimal, ws_roa: Decimal, ws_total_equity: Decimal, ws_roe: Decimal, ws_interest_expense: Decimal, ws_interest_income: Decimal, ws_earning_assets: Decimal, ws_nim: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate financial KPIs."""
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_equity > 0:
        ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100
    if ws_interest_expense > 0:
        ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10(ws_total_trans_count: int, ws_error_count: int, ws_error_rate: Decimal, ws_within_sla_count: int, ws_total_cases: int, ws_sla_compliance: Decimal, ws_fcr_count: int, ws_total_calls: int, ws_first_call_resolution: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate operational KPIs."""
    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10(ws_active_customers: int, ws_churned_customers: int, ws_churn_rate: Decimal, ws_marketing_spend: Decimal, ws_new_customers: int, ws_acquisition_cost: Decimal, ws_avg_revenue_per_customer: Decimal, ws_avg_customer_tenure: Decimal, ws_lifetime_value: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate customer KPIs."""
    if ws_active_customers > 0:
        ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100
    ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure

def generate_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Generate dashboards."""
    create_executive_dashboard_C10()
    create_operations_dashboard_C10()
    create_risk_dashboard_C10()

def create_executive_dashboard_C10(dash_title: str, ws_total_revenue: Any, dash_revenue: Any, ws_net_income: Any, dash_net_income: Any, ws_roa: Any, dash_roa: Any, ws_roe: Any, dash_roe: Any, ws_active_customers: Any, dash_customers: Any, ws_exec_dashboard: Any, dashboard_record: Any) -> None:
    """Create the executive dashboard."""
    dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    dash_revenue = ws_total_revenue
    dash_net_income = ws_net_income
    dash_roa = ws_roa
    dash_roe = ws_roe
    dash_customers = ws_active_customers
    dashboard_record = ws_exec_dashboard

def create_operations_dashboard_C10(dash_title: str, ws_total_trans_count: Any, dash_trans_count: Any, ws_avg_response_time: Any, dash_avg_response: Any, ws_error_rate: Any, dash_error_rate: Any, ws_sla_compliance: Any, dash_sla_pct: Any, ws_ops_dashboard: Any, dashboard_record: Any) -> None:
    """Create the operations dashboard."""
    dash_title = 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD'
    dash_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    dash_avg_response = ws_avg_response_time
    dash_error_rate = ws_error_rate
    dash_sla_pct = ws_sla_compliance
    dashboard_record = ws_ops_dashboard

def create_risk_dashboard_C10(dash_title: str, ws_fraud_score: Any, dash_fraud_score: Any, ws_npl_ratio: Any, dash_npl: Any, ws_capital_ratio: Any, dash_capital: Any, ws_liquidity_ratio: Any, dash_liquidity: Any, ws_risk_dashboard: Any, dashboard_record: Any) -> None:
    """Create the risk dashboard."""
    dash_title = 'RISK DASHBOARD'
    dash_fraud_score = ws_fraud_score
    dash_npl = ws_npl_ratio
    dash_capital = ws_capital_ratio
    dash_liquidity = ws_liquidity_ratio
    dashboard_record = ws_risk_dashboard

def export_data_C10() -> None:
    """Export data in various formats."""
    export_csv_C10()
    export_xml_C10()
    export_json_C10()

def export_csv_C10(csv_export_file: Any, ws_csv_header: str, ws_csv_line: str, daily_summary_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, daily_date: Any, daily_trans_count: Any, daily_trans_amount: Any, daily_deposits: Any, daily_withdrawals: Any, csv_record: Any, ws_eof_flag: str) -> None:
    """Export data to CSV format."""
    try:
        csv_export_file = open("output.csv", "w")
        ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
        csv_export_file.write(ws_csv_header + '
')
        ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
            try:
                ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
                if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                    ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
                else:
                    ws_csv_line = f"{daily_date},{daily_trans_count},{daily_trans_amount},{daily_deposits},{daily_withdrawals}"
                    csv_export_file.write(ws_csv_line + '
')
            except Exception:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        csv_export_file.close()
        ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error exporting CSV: {e}")

def export_xml_C10(xml_export_file: Any, ws_xml_line: str, daily_summary_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, xml_record: Any) -> None:
    """Export data to XML format."""
    try:
        xml_export_file = open("output.xml", "w")
        ws_xml_line = '<?xml version="1.0"?>'
        xml_export_file.write(ws_xml_line + '
')
        ws_xml_line = '<DailySummaries>'
        xml_export_file.write(ws_xml_line + '
')
        write_xml_records_C10(xml_export_file, daily_summary_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, xml_record)
        ws_xml_line = '</DailySummaries>'
        xml_export_file.write(ws_xml_line + '
')
        xml_export_file.close()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error exporting XML: {e}")

def write_xml_records_C10(xml_export_file: Any, daily_summary_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, xml_record: Any, ws_eof_flag: str) -> None:
    """Write XML records."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                format_xml_record_C10(xml_export_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, xml_record)
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record_C10(xml_export_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, xml_record: Any, daily_date: Any, daily_trans_count: Any) -> None:
    """Format a single XML record."""
    ws_xml_line = '<Summary>'
    xml_export_file.write(ws_xml_line + '
')
    ws_xml_line = f'<Date>{daily_date}</Date>'
    xml_export_file.write(ws_xml_line + '
')
    ws_xml_line = f'<TransCount>{daily_trans_count}</TransCount>'
    xml_export_file.write(ws_xml_line + '
')
    ws_xml_line = '</Summary>'
    xml_export_file.write(ws_xml_line + '
')

def export_json_C10(json_export_file: Any, ws_json_line: str, daily_summary_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, json_record: Any) -> None:
    """Export data to JSON format."""
    try:
        json_export_file = open("output.json", "w")
        ws_json_line = '{"dailySummaries":['
        json_export_file.write(ws_json_line + '
')
        write_json_records_C10(json_export_file, daily_summary_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, json_record)
        ws_json_line = ']}'
        json_export_file.write(ws_json_line + '
')
        json_export_file.close()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error exporting JSON: {e}")

def write_json_records_C10(json_export_file: Any, daily_summary_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, json_record: Any, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_first_record: str) -> None:
    """Write JSON records."""
    ws_first_record = 'N'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                format_json_record_C10(json_export_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, json_record, ws_first_record)
                ws_first_record = 'Y'
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record_C10(json_export_file: Any, ws_daily_sum_rec: Any, json_record: Any, ws_first_record: str, ws_json_comma: str, daily_date: Any, daily_trans_count: Any, daily_trans_amount: Any) -> None:
    """Format a single JSON record."""
    if ws_first_record == 'Y':
        ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        ws_json_comma = ''
    ws_json_line = f'{ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{daily_date}","transCount":{daily_trans_count},"transAmount":{daily_trans_amount}}}'
    json_export_file.write(ws_json_line + '
')

def account_maintenance_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    dormant_account_check_C10()
    escheatment_processing_C10()
    account_closure_C10()
    account_reactivation_C10()

def dormant_account_check_C10(account_file: Any, ws_account_rec: Any, ws_eof_flag: str) -> None:
    """Check for dormant accounts."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = account_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_account_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                check_activity_C10()
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_activity_C10(ws_process_date: Any, acct_last_activity: Any, ws_days_inactive: int, acct_status: str, ws_account_rec: Any) -> None:
    """Check account activity."""
    ws_days_inactive = int(ws_process_date) - int(acct_last_activity)
    if ws_days_inactive > 365:
        acct_status = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10(acct_status, ws_process_date, ws_account_rec)

def mark_dormant_C10(acct_status: str, ws_process_date: Any, ws_account_rec: Any, acct_status_desc: str, acct_dormant_date: Any) -> None:
    """Mark account as dormant."""
    acct_status_desc = 'DORMANT'
    acct_dormant_date = ws_process_date
    #rewrite account record
    send_dormant_notice_C10()

def send_dormant_notice_C10(ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Send a dormant account notice."""
    ws_notif_type = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10()

def escheatment_processing_C10(account_file: Any, ws_account_rec: Any, acct_status: str, ws_eof_flag: str) -> None:
    """Process accounts for escheatment."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = account_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_account_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                if acct_status == 'D':
                    check_escheatment_C10()
        except Exception:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_escheatment_C10(ws_process_date: Any, acct_dormant_date: Any, ws_dormant_years: Decimal, ws_escheat_years: int) -> None:
    """Check if an account is eligible for escheatment."""
    ws_dormant_years = (int(ws_process_date) - int(acct_dormant_date)) / 365
    if ws_dormant_years >= ws_escheat_years:
        escheat_account_C10()

def escheat_account_C10(acct_status: str, acct_balance: Decimal, ws_escheat_amount: Decimal, ws_account_rec: Any, acct_id: Any, acct_owner_name: Any, acct_owner_address: Any, ws_process_date: Any) -> None:
    """Escheat an account."""
    acct_status = 'E'
    ws_escheat_amount = acct_balance
    acct_balance = Decimal('0')
    create_escheat_record_C10(acct_id, ws_escheat_amount, ws_process_date, acct_owner_name, acct_owner_address)
    #rewrite account record

def create_escheat_record_C10(acct_id: Any, ws_escheat_amount: Decimal, ws_process_date: Any, acct_owner_name: Any, acct_owner_address: Any, ws_escheat_record: Any, escheat_account: Any, escheat_amount: Decimal, escheat_date: Any, escheat_owner: Any, escheat_address: Any, escheat_record: Any) -> None:
    """Create an escheat record."""
    ws_escheat_record = {}
    escheat_account = acct_id
    escheat_amount = ws_escheat_amount
    escheat_date = ws_process_date
    escheat_owner = acct_owner_name
    escheat_address = acct_owner_address
    escheat_record = ws_escheat_record
    #write escheat record

def account_closure_C10(ws_close_request: str, acct_balance: Decimal, acct_pending_trans: int, acct_loan_link: str, ws_closure_valid: str, ws_closure_reject: str, ws_account_rec: Any, ws_final_balance: Decimal, acct_id: Any, acct_owner_name: Any, ws_process_date: Any) -> None:
    """Process account closures."""
    if ws_close_request == 'Y':
        validate_closure_C10(acct_balance, acct_pending_trans, acct_loan_link, ws_closure_valid, ws_closure_reject)
        if ws_closure_valid == 'Y':
            ws_final_balance = acct_balance
            process_closure_C10(ws_final_balance, acct_id, acct_owner_name, ws_process_date, ws_account_rec)
        else:
            reject_closure_C10(ws_closure_reject)

def validate_closure_C10(acct_balance: Decimal, acct_pending_trans: int, acct_loan_link: str, ws_closure_valid: str, ws_closure_reject: str) -> None:
    """Validate account closure requests."""
    ws_closure_valid = 'Y'
    if acct_balance < 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'NEGATIVE BALANCE'
    if acct_pending_trans > 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'PENDING TRANSACTIONS'
    if acct_loan_link != '':
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'LINKED LOAN EXISTS'

def process_closure_C10(ws_final_balance: Decimal, acct_id: Any, acct_owner_name: Any, ws_process_date: Any, ws_account_rec: Any, acct_status: str, acct_close_date: Any) -> None:
    """Process account closure."""
    disburse_balance_C10(ws_final_balance, acct_id, acct_owner_name)
    acct_status = 'C'
    acct_close_date = ws_process_date
    #rewrite account record
    archive_account_C10(ws_process_date, ws_account_rec)

def disburse_balance_C10(ws_final_balance: Decimal, acct_id: Any, acct_owner_name: Any, ws_check_record: Any, check_from_account: Any, check_amount: Decimal, check_memo: str, check_payee: Any, check_record: Any) -> None:
    """Disburse the remaining balance of a closed account."""
    if ws_final_balance > 0:
        ws_check_record = {}
        check_from_account = acct_id
        check_amount = ws_final_balance
        check_memo = 'ACCOUNT CLOSURE'
        check_payee = acct_owner_name
        check_record = ws_check_record
        #write check record

def archive_account_C10(ws_process_date: Any, ws_account_rec: Any, ws_archive_record: Any, archive_account_data: Any, archive_date: Any, archive_retention: int, archive_record: Any) -> None:
    """Archive account data after closure."""
    ws_archive_record = {}
    archive_account_data = ws_account_rec
    archive_date = ws_process_date
    archive_retention = int(ws_process_date) + 2555
    archive_record = ws_archive_record
    #write archive record

def reject_closure_C10(ws_closure_reject: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Reject account closure request."""
    ws_notif_type = 'closure_reject'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Closure rejected: {ws_closure_reject}'
    send_notification_C10()

def account_reactivation_C10(ws_reactivate_request: str, acct_status: str, ws_react_valid: str, ws_react_reject: str, ws_days_since_close: int, ws_account_rec: Any, ws_process_date: Any) -> None:
    """Process account reactivations."""
    if ws_reactivate_request == 'Y':
        validate_reactivation_C10(acct_status, ws_react_valid, ws_react_reject, ws_days_since_close)
        if ws_react_valid == 'Y':
            process_reactivation_C10(ws_process_date, ws_account_rec)

def validate_reactivation_C10(acct_status: str, ws_react_valid: str, ws_react_reject: str, ws_days_since_close: int) -> None:
    """Validate account reactivation requests."""
    ws_react_valid = 'Y'
    if acct_status == 'E':
        ws_react_valid = 'N'
        ws_react_reject = 'ACCOUNT ESCHEATED'
    if acct_status == 'C':
        if ws_days_since_close > 90:
            ws_react_valid = 'N'
            ws_react_reject = 'CLOSURE PERIOD EXCEEDED'

def process_reactivation_C10(ws_process_date: Any, ws_account_rec: Any, acct_status: str, acct_react_date: Any, acct_dormant_date: Any) -> None:
    """Process account reactivation."""
    acct_status = 'A'
    acct_react_date = ws_process_date
    acct_dormant_date = ''
    #rewrite account record
    send_reactivation_confirm_C10()

def send_reactivation_confirm_C10(ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Send a reactivation confirmation."""
    ws_notif_type = 'REACTIVATION'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Your account has been reactivated'
    send_notification_C10()

def card_management_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    card_issuance_C10()
    card_activation_C10()
    pin_management_C10()
    card_replacement_C10()
    card_blocking_C10()

def card_issuance_C10() -> None:
    """Process card issuance."""
    generate_card_number_C10()
    set_card_limits_C10()
    assign_network_C10()
    create_card_record_C10()

def generate_card_number_C10(ws_card_prefix: str, ws_bin_number: Any, ws_card_bin: Any, ws_card_seq: Decimal, ws_card_number_temp: str, ws_luhn_check: Any, ws_card_number: str) -> None:
    """Generate a card number."""
    ws_card_prefix = '4'
    ws_card_bin = ws_bin_number
    ws_card_seq = Decimal(str

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Blocks a card."""
    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    CARD_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_CARD_RECORD
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = f'Your card has been blocked: {WS_BLOCK_REASON}'
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Processes a wire transfer."""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Validates a wire transfer request."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == SPACES:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """Screens a wire transfer against OFAC."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    #call 'OFACSRCH' using OFAC_REQUEST OFAC_RESPONSE
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    #call 'OFACSRCH' using OFAC_REQUEST OFAC_RESPONSE
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Processes a wire transfer."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Debits the originator's account."""
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def create_wire_message_C11() -> None:
    """Creates a SWIFT wire message."""
    #INITIALIZE WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE
    SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103'
    SWIFT_TXN_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    SWIFT_VALUE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_DATE
    SWIFT_CURRENCY  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_CURRENCY
    SWIFT_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST  = None  # TODO: was WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME
    SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    SWIFT_BENEF_CUST  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC
    SWIFT_REMIT_INFO  = None  # TODO: was WS_PURPOSE

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Transmits a wire message."""
    #call 'SWIFTSEND' using WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE  WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

def record_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Records a wire transfer."""
    #INITIALIZE WS_WIRE_RECORD
    WIRE_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    WIRE_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WIRE_STATUS  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_STATUS
    WIRE_FROM_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_TO_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    #WRITE WIRE_RECORD FROM WS_WIRE_RECORD

def reverse_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Reverses a debit."""
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def send_confirmation_C11() -> None:
    """Sends a wire transfer confirmation."""
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_confirm'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Wire transfer {WS_WIRE_REF} completed'
    send_notification_C11()

def reject_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Rejects a wire transfer."""
    WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED'
    #INITIALIZE WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC
    REJECT_WIRE_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    REJECT_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REJECT
    REJECT_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    #WRITE WIRE_REJECT_RECORD FROM WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_rejected'
    send_notification_C11()

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:
    """Processes an ACH file."""
    receive_ach_file_C11()
    validate_ach_entries_C11()
    process_ach_credits_C11()
    process_ach_debits_C11()
    generate_ach_return_C11()

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """Receives an ACH file."""
    #OPEN INPUT ACH_INPUT_FILE
    #READ ACH_INPUT_FILE INTO WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_FILE_ID
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_CREATION_DATE
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ENTRY_COUNT

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Validates ACH entries."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ ACH_INPUT_FILE INTO WS_ACH_ENTRY
        #AT END
        #   WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        #NOT AT END
        validate_single_entry_C11()
        #
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Validates a single ACH entry."""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == SPACES:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        global WS_VALID_ENTRIES
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        global WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """Processes ACH credits."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ ACH_INPUT_FILE INTO WS_ACH_ENTRY
        #AT END
        #    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        #NOT AT END
        if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('22', '23', '32', '33'):
            apply_credit_C11()
        #
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_credit_C11() -> None:
    """Applies an ACH credit."""
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """Processes ACH debits."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ ACH_INPUT_FILE INTO WS_ACH_ENTRY
        #AT END
        #   WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        #NOT AT END
        if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('27', '28', '37', '38'):
            apply_debit_C11()
        #
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Applies an ACH debit."""
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Generates an ACH return file."""
    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

def create_return_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Creates an ACH return entry."""
    #INITIALIZE WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY
    RETURN_ORIG_TRACE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_TRACE_NUMBER
    RETURN_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    RETURN_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    RETURN_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT
    WS_RETURN_COUNT += 1
    #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY

def create_return_file_C11() -> None:
    """Creates an ACH return file."""
    #OPEN OUTPUT ACH_RETURN_FILE
    write_return_header_C11()
    write_return_entries_C11()
    write_return_trailer_C11()
    #CLOSE ACH_RETURN_FILE

def write_return_header_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the return file header."""
    #INITIALIZE WS_RETURN_HEADER
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '1'
    RETURN_PRIORITY_CODE = '01'
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_DEST  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_ROUTING
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_ORIGIN  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID
    #RETURN_FILE_DATE = FUNCTION current_date
    #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_RETURN_HEADER

def write_return_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the return file entries."""
    global WS_RETURN_IDX
    WS_RETURN_IDX = 0
    while WS_RETURN_IDX > WS_RETURN_COUNT:
        #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_RETURN_ENTRY(WS_RETURN_IDX)
        WS_RETURN_IDX += 1

def write_return_trailer_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the return file trailer."""
    #INITIALIZE WS_RETURN_TRAILER
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '9'
    RETURN_ENTRY_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_COUNT
    RETURN_TOTAL_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_TOTAL
    #WRITE ACH_RETURN_RECORD FROM WS_RETURN_TRAILER

def statement_generation_C11() -> None:
    """Generates account statements."""
    prepare_statement_data_C11()
    generate_account_summary_C11()
    generate_transaction_detail_C11()
    calculate_statement_totals_C11()
    format_statement_C11()
    deliver_statement_C11()

def prepare_statement_data_C11() -> None:
    """Prepares data for statement generation."""
    #WS_STMT_DATE = FUNCTION current_date
    #WS_STMT_START_DATE = FUNCTION integer_of_date(WS_STMT_DATE) - 30
    WS_STMT_END_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DATE
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT = 0
    WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")

def generate_account_summary_C11() -> None:
    """Generates the account summary section of the statement."""
    #INITIALIZE WS_STMT_SUMMARY
    STMT_ACCOUNT_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_ID
    STMT_ACCOUNT_TYPE  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_TYPE
    STMT_CUSTOMER_NAME  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_OWNER_NAME
    STMT_CUSTOMER_ADDR  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_OWNER_ADDRESS
    STMT_OPENING_BAL  = None  # TODO: was WS_OPENING_BALANCE
    STMT_CLOSING_BAL  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE

def generate_transaction_detail_C11() -> None:
    """Generates the transaction detail section of the statement."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #READ TRANSACTION_HISTORY INTO WS_TRANS_HIST_REC
        #AT END
        #   WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        #NOT AT END
        if HIST_ACCOUNT == ACCT_ID:
            if HIST_DATE >= WS_STMT_START_DATE:
                add_transaction_line_C11()
        #
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def add_transaction_line_C11() -> None:
    """Adds a transaction line to the statement."""
    global WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT, WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL, WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT += 1
    STMT_TRANS_DATE  = None  # TODO: was HIST_DATE
    STMT_TRANS_DESC  = None  # TODO: was HIST_DESC
    STMT_TRANS_AMT  = None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT
    STMT_TRANS_BAL  = None  # TODO: was HIST_BALANCE
    if HIST_TYPE == 'C':
        WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT

def calculate_statement_totals_C11() -> None:
    """Calculates statement totals."""
    STMT_TOTAL_CREDITS = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL
    STMT_TOTAL_DEBITS  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    STMT_NET_CHANGE = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL - WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    STMT_TRANS_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT
    if WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        #STMT_AVG_DAILY_BAL = WS_TOTAL_DAILY_BALANCES / 30
        pass

def format_statement_C11() -> None:
    """Formats the statement."""
    create_header_C11()
    create_summary_section_C11()
    create_transaction_list_C11()
    create_footer_C11()

def create_header_C11() -> None:
    """Creates the statement header."""
    WS_STMT_LINE = ' '
    #STRING 'ACCOUNT STATEMENT' DELIMITED SIZE  ' - ' DELIMITED SIZE WS_STMT_DATE DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS_STMT_LINE
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = '-' * len(WS_STMT_LINE)
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE

def create_summary_section_C11() -> None:
    """Creates the summary section of the statement."""
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'Account: {STMT_ACCOUNT_NUMBER}'
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'Customer: {STMT_CUSTOMER_NAME}'
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'Opening Balance: ${STMT_OPENING_BAL}'
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'Closing Balance: ${STMT_CLOSING_BAL}'
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE

def create_transaction_list_C11() -> None:
    """Creates the transaction list section of the statement."""
    WS_STMT_LINE = 'DATE       DESCRIPTION                    AMOUNT'
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = '-' * len(WS_STMT_LINE)
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    global WS_STMT_IDX
    WS_STMT_IDX = 1
    while WS_STMT_IDX > WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT:
        #STRING STMT_TRANS_DATE(WS_STMT_IDX) DELIMITED SIZE '  ' DELIMITED SIZE STMT_TRANS_DESC(WS_STMT_IDX) DELIMITED SIZE '  $' DELIMITED SIZE STMT_TRANS_AMT(WS_STMT_IDX) DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS_STMT_LINE
        #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
        WS_STMT_IDX += 1

def create_footer_C11() -> None:
    """Creates the statement footer."""
    WS_STMT_LINE = '-' * len(WS_STMT_LINE)
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'Total Credits: ${STMT_TOTAL_CREDITS}'
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'Total Debits: ${STMT_TOTAL_DEBITS}'
    #WRITE STATEMENT_RECORD FROM WS_STMT_LINE

def deliver_statement_C11() -> None:
    """Delivers the statement based on delivery preference."""
    if WS_DELIVERY_PREF == 'PAPER':
        print_statement_C11()
    elif WS_DELIVERY_PRE

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """Validates stop request."""
    WS_STOP_VALID: str = ""
    WS_STOP_REJECT: str = ""
    WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def safe_deposit_box_C12() -> None:
    """Safe deposit box procedures."""
    box_rental_C12()
    box_access_C12()
    box_drilling_C12()
    box_billing_C12()

def box_rental_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box rental requests."""
    if WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
        check_availability_C12()
        if WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

def check_availability_C12() -> None:
    """Checks box availability."""
    WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
    WS_BOX_IDX = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'A':
            if BOX_SIZE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                WS_ASSIGNED_BOX  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_IDX
                break
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1

def assign_box_C12() -> None:
    """Assigns a safe deposit box."""
    BOX_STATUS[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R'
    BOX_RENTER[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1]  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    BOX_RENTAL_DATE[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1]  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE

def create_rental_agreement_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a rental agreement."""
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT: WsRentalAgreementC12 = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    RENTAL_RECORD: RentalRecordC12 = RentalRecordC12()

    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was WS_ASSIGNED_BOX
    RENTAL_CUSTOMER  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    RENTAL_START_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE = WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[int(WS_REQUESTED_SIZE)]
    RENTAL_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was RENTAL_RECORD

def box_access_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box access requests."""
    if WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
        verify_renter_C12()
        if WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            log_access_C12()
            escort_to_vault_C12()

def verify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Verifies the renter."""
    WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
    if BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1] == WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
        if WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            if WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

def log_access_C12() -> None:
    """Logs the box access."""
    WS_ACCESS_LOG: WsAccessLogC12 = WsAccessLogC12()
    ACCESS_LOG_RECORD: AccessLogRecordC12 = AccessLogRecordC12()
    WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLogC12()
    ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER
    ACCESS_CUSTOMER  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    ACCESS_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ACCESS_TIME = 0
    ACCESS_TYPE = 'ENTRY'
    ACCESS_LOG_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was ACCESS_LOG_RECORD

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """Escorts the renter to the vault."""
    WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
    print(WS_DISPLAY_MSG)

def box_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box drilling requests."""
    if WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
        validate_drilling_auth_C12()
        if WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling_C12()
            notify_renter_C12()

def validate_drilling_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Validates drilling authorization."""
    WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
    if WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
    if WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
    if WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
        if WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
            WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

def schedule_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Schedules the drilling."""
    WS_DRILLING_RECORD: WsDrillingRecordC12 = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    DRILLING_RECORD: DrillingRecordC12 = DrillingRecordC12()
    WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    DRILL_BOX_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER
    DRILL_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_DRILLING_REASON
    DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE = int(WS_PROCESS_DATE + 30)
    DRILLING_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was DRILLING_RECORD

def notify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Notifies the renter."""
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE: str = ""
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL: str = ""
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT: str = ""
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'box_drilling'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification_C12()

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box billing."""
    WS_BOX_IDX = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R':
            if BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee_C12()
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1

def charge_annual_fee_C12() -> None:
    """Charges the annual fee."""
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE: str = ""
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL: str = ""
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT: str = ""
    WS_CUSTOMER_ID = BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
    WS_FEE_AMOUNT = BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE - WS_FEE_AMOUNT
    update_account_C12()
    BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """Merchant services procedures."""
    process_authorization_C12()
    capture_transaction_C12()
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12()

def process_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """Processes authorization."""
    validate_card_C12()
    if WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_C12()
        if WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_C12()
            if WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12() -> None:
    """Validates the card."""
    WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
    check_luhn_C12()
    if WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
        check_expiry_C12()
        if WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
            check_cvv_C12()
            if WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

def check_luhn_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the Luhn algorithm."""
    WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
    WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
    while WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
        WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])
        if (17 - WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
            WS_LUHN_DIGIT = WS_LUHN_DIGIT * 2
            if WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                WS_LUHN_DIGIT = WS_LUHN_DIGIT - 9
        WS_LUHN_SUM = WS_LUHN_SUM + WS_LUHN_DIGIT
        WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1
    if WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

def check_expiry_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the expiry date."""
    if WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= WS_PROCESS_DATE:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

def check_cvv_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the CVV."""
    WS_CVV_VALID: str = ""
    WS_CVV_RESULT: str = ""
    WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER: str = ""
    WS_AUTH_CVV: str = ""
    if WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

def check_fraud_score_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the fraud score."""
    WS_AUTH_REQUEST: WsAuthRequestC12 = WsAuthRequestC12()
    WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE: str = ""
    FRAUD_SCORE_C12: FraudScoreC12 = FraudScoreC12()
    if FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE  = None  # TODO: was FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

def check_available_credit_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the available credit."""
    WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC: WsCardAccountRecC12 = WsCardAccountRecC12()
    CARD_ACCOUNT_FILE: CardAccountFileC12 = CardAccountFileC12()
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC  = None  # TODO: was WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC
    if WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

def approve_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Approves authorization."""
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
    generate_auth_code_C12()
    WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT = WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT - WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
    record_authorization_C12()

def generate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """Generates authorization code."""
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE: int = 0
    WS_AUTH_CODE = int(0 * 999999)
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CODE

def process_settlement_C12() -> None:
    """Processes settlement."""
    batch_transactions_C12()
    calculate_fees_C12()
    create_funding_record_C12()
    send_settlement_file_C12()

def batch_transactions_C12() -> None:
    """Batches transactions."""
    WS_CAPTURE_REC: WsCaptureRecC12 = WsCaptureRecC12()
    CAPTURE_FILE: Any = None
    WS_BATCH_TOTAL = 0
    WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        try:
            WS_CAPTURE_REC  = None  # TODO: was CAPTURE_FILE
            if CAPTURE_SETTLED == 'N':
                WS_BATCH_TOTAL = WS_BATCH_TOTAL + CAPTURE_AMOUNT
                WS_BATCH_COUNT = WS_BATCH_COUNT + 1
                CAPTURE_SETTLED = 'Y'
                WS_CAPTURE_REC  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPTURE_REC
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def calculate_fees_C12() -> None:
    """Calculates fees."""
    WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0175")
    WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0015")
    WS_PROCESSOR_FEE = WS_BATCH_COUNT * Decimal("0.10")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES = WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE + WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE + WS_PROCESSOR_FEE

def create_funding_record_C12() -> None:
    """Creates funding record."""
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD: WsFundingRecordC12 = WsFundingRecordC12()
    FUNDING_RECORD: FundingRecordC12 = FundingRecordC12()

    WS_NET_FUNDING = WS_BATCH_TOTAL - WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD = WsFundingRecordC12()
    FUNDING_MERCHANT  = None  # TODO: was WS_MERCHANT_ID
    FUNDING_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_NET_FUNDING
    FUNDING_FEES  = None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES
    FUNDING_DATE = int(WS_PROCESS_DATE + 2)
    FUNDING_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was FUNDING_RECORD

def send_settlement_file_C12() -> None:
    """Sends settlement file."""
    SETTLEMENT_FILE: Any = None
    settlement_header_C12()

    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        try:
            WS_CAPTURE_REC  = None  # TODO: was CAPTURE_FILE
            if CAPTURE_SETTLED == 'Y':
                WS_SETTLE_DETAIL = WsSettleDetailC12()
                SET

    except Exception:

def logging_utilities_C13() -> None:
    """Calls logging functions."""
    log_info_C13()
    log_warning_C13()
    log_error_C13()

def log_info_C13() -> None:
    """Logs an info message."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'INFO'
    LOG_MESSAGE  = None  # TODO: was WS_LOG_MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_LOG_ENTRY = f"{LOG_LEVEL} - {LOG_TIMESTAMP} - {LOG_MESSAGE}"
    print(WS_LOG_ENTRY)

def log_warning_C13() -> None:
    """Logs a warning message."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'WARN'
    LOG_MESSAGE  = None  # TODO: was WS_LOG_MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_LOG_ENTRY = f"{LOG_LEVEL} - {LOG_TIMESTAMP} - {LOG_MESSAGE}"
    print(WS_LOG_ENTRY)

def log_error_C13() -> None:
    """Logs an error message."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'ERROR'
    LOG_MESSAGE  = None  # TODO: was WS_LOG_MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_LOG_ENTRY = f"{LOG_LEVEL} - {LOG_TIMESTAMP} - {LOG_MESSAGE}"
    print(WS_LOG_ENTRY)

def error_handling_C13() -> None:
    """Handles errors."""
    format_error_C13()
    display_error_C13()
    write_error_log_C13()

def format_error_C13() -> None:
    """Formats an error message."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR, WS_ERROR_CODE, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_FORMATTED_ERROR = f"ERROR: {WS_ERROR_CODE} - {WS_ERROR_MSG}"

def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """Displays an error message."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR
    print(WS_FORMATTED_ERROR)

def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """Writes an error log."""
    global WS_ERROR_LOG_REC, ERR_LOG_CODE, ERR_LOG_MSG, ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_PROGRAM_NAME, WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME
    WS_ERROR_LOG_REC = ""
    ERR_LOG_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_CODE
    ERR_LOG_MSG  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_MSG
    ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_PROGRAM_NAME = "current_program"
    WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME = "current_paragraph"
    print(f"Writing error log: {ERR_LOG_CODE} - {ERR_LOG_MSG} - {ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP} - {WS_PROGRAM_NAME} - {WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME}")

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manages treasury functions."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the cash position."""
    global WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_CASH_POSITION = Decimal("0.00")
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the vault cash."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_VAULT_REC, WS_CASH_POSITION, VAULT_BALANCE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # Simulate reading from a file
            WS_VAULT_REC = "Vault record data"
            VAULT_BALANCE = Decimal("10000.00")  # Example value
            WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was VAULT_BALANCE
        except FileNotFoundError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error reading vault cash file: {e}")
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            pass  # No action needed for successful read
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the fed account."""
    global WS_FED_BALANCE, WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_FED_BALANCE = Decimal("50000.00")
    WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was WS_FED_BALANCE

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the correspondent balances."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CORR_REC, WS_CASH_POSITION, CORR_BALANCE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # Simulate reading from a file
            WS_CORR_REC = "Correspondent record data"
            CORR_BALANCE = Decimal("25000.00")  # Example value
            WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was CORR_BALANCE
        except FileNotFoundError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error reading correspondent file: {e}")
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            pass  # No action needed for successful read
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects cash flows."""
    global WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS, WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS, WS_NET_POSITION, WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    WS_NET_POSITION = WS_CASH_POSITION + WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS - WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Projects loan payments."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_LOAN_PMT_REC, WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS, LOAN_PMT_DATE, WS_PROJECTION_DATE, LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # Simulate reading from a file
            WS_LOAN_PMT_REC = "Loan payment record data"
            LOAN_PMT_DATE = datetime.now()  # Example date
            LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT = Decimal("5000.00")  # Example value
            WS_PROJECTION_DATE = datetime.now()
            if LOAN_PMT_DATE <= WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS += None  # TODO: was LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT
        except FileNotFoundError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error reading loan schedule file: {e}")
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            pass  # No action needed for successful read
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects deposit flows."""
    global WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS, WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS, WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS, WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS, WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS, WS_PROJECTION_DAYS, WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS
    WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS = Decimal("100000.00")
    WS_PROJECTION_DAYS = 30
    WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS = Decimal("75000.00")
    WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS = WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS = WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS += WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS
    WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS += WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS

            # Simulate reading from a file
            WS_INV_REC = "Investment record data"
            INV_MATURITY_DATE = datetime.now()  # Example date
            INV_PAR_VALUE = Decimal("1000000.00")  # Example value
            WS_PROJECTION_DATE = datetime.now()
            if INV_MATURITY_DATE <= WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS += None  # TODO: was INV_PAR_VALUE

def capital_management_C14() -> None:
    """Capital management procedures."""
    calculate_capital_ratios_C14()
    risk_weighted_assets_C14()
    capital_planning_C14()
    stress_testing_C14()

def calculate_capital_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate capital ratios."""
    calculate_tier1_C14()
    calculate_tier2_C14()
    calculate_ratios_C14()

def calculate_tier1_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate Tier 1 capital."""
    global ws_tier1_capital  # Assuming this is a global variable
    ws_tier1_capital = 0
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_common_stock  # Assuming ws_common_stock is defined elsewhere
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_retained_earnings  # Assuming ws_retained_earnings is defined elsewhere
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_aoci  # Assuming ws_aoci is defined elsewhere
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_goodwill  # Assuming ws_goodwill is defined elsewhere
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_intangibles  # Assuming ws_intangibles is defined elsewhere
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_dta_deduction  # Assuming ws_dta_deduction is defined elsewhere

def calculate_tier2_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate Tier 2 capital."""
    global ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital
    ws_tier2_capital = 0
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_sub_debt  # Assuming ws_sub_debt is defined elsewhere
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_alll_eligible  # Assuming ws_alll_eligible is defined elsewhere
    ws_total_capital = ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital

def calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate capital ratios."""
    global ws_cet1_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_leverage_ratio
    if ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0:
        ws_cet1_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
        ws_capital_ratio = (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_leverage_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100

def risk_weighted_assets_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = 0
    credit_rwa_C14()
    market_rwa_C14()
    operational_rwa_C14()

def credit_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate credit risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets
    ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * 0.00  # Assuming ws_cash_position is defined elsewhere
    ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * 0.00  # Assuming ws_govt_securities is defined elsewhere
    ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * 0.20  # Assuming ws_bank_deposits is defined elsewhere
    ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * 0.50  # Assuming ws_residential_mortgages is defined elsewhere
    ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * 1.00  # Assuming ws_commercial_loans is defined elsewhere
    ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * 1.00  # Assuming ws_consumer_loans is defined elsewhere
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_cash_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_govt_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_bank_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_mortgage_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_commercial_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_consumer_rwa

def market_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate market risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets
    ws_market_rwa = ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor  # Assuming ws_trading_assets and ws_market_risk_factor are defined elsewhere
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_market_rwa

def operational_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate operational risk-weighted assets."""
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets
    ws_operational_rwa = ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * 12.5  # Assuming ws_gross_income and ws_operational_factor are defined elsewhere
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_operational_rwa

def capital_planning_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    project_capital_needs_C14()
    identify_capital_actions_C14()
    update_capital_plan_C14()

def project_capital_needs_C14() -> None:
    """Project capital needs."""
    global ws_projected_rwa, ws_required_capital, ws_capital_gap
    ws_projected_rwa = ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + ws_growth_rate)  # Assuming ws_risk_weighted_assets and ws_growth_rate are defined elsewhere
    ws_required_capital = ws_projected_rwa * ws_target_ratio / 100  # Assuming ws_target_ratio is defined elsewhere
    ws_capital_gap = ws_required_capital - ws_total_capital  # Assuming ws_total_capital is defined elsewhere

def identify_capital_actions_C14() -> None:
    """Identify appropriate capital actions."""
    global ws_capital_action
    if ws_capital_gap > 0:
        if ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:  # Assuming ws_capital_gap and ws_retained_earnings_proj are defined elsewhere
            ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:  # Assuming ws_sub_debt_capacity is defined elsewhere
            ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

def update_capital_plan_C14() -> None:
    """Update the capital plan."""
    ws_plan_update_date = " " # Placeholder for current_date function
    plan_recommended_action = ws_capital_action  # Assuming ws_capital_action is defined elsewhere
    plan_gap_amount = ws_capital_gap  # Assuming ws_capital_gap is defined elsewhere
    capital_plan_record = ws_capital_plan  # Assuming ws_capital_plan and CAPITAL_PLAN_RECORD are defined elsewhere

def stress_testing_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    run_baseline_C14()
    run_adverse_C14()
    run_severely_adverse_C14()
    compile_results_C14()

def run_baseline_C14() -> None:
    """Run the baseline stress test scenario."""
    global ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline
    ws_scenario_name = 'BASELINE'
    ws_rate_shock = 0.00
    ws_gdp_change = 2.50
    ws_unemployment_rate = 4.00
    ws_housing_decline = 0.00
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Run the adverse stress test scenario."""
    global ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline
    ws_scenario_name = 'ADVERSE'
    ws_rate_shock = 2.00
    ws_gdp_change = -1.50
    ws_unemployment_rate = 7.00
    ws_housing_decline = -15.00
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_severely_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Run the severely adverse stress test scenario."""
    global ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline
    ws_scenario_name = 'severely_adverse'
    ws_rate_shock = 3.00
    ws_gdp_change = -6.00
    ws_unemployment_rate = 10.00
    ws_housing_decline = -30.00
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def compile_results_C14() -> None:
    """Compile the stress test results."""
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')
    if ws_stress_pass_fail == 'FAIL':  # Assuming ws_stress_pass_fail is defined elsewhere
        remediation_actions_C14()

def calculate_stress_impact_C14() -> None:
    """Calculate the impact of a stress test scenario."""
    global ws_credit_losses, ws_market_losses, ws_stress_losses, ws_stressed_capital, ws_stressed_ratio, ws_stress_pass_fail
    ws_credit_losses = ws_loan_portfolio * ws_stress_lgd * ws_stress_pd  # Assuming ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, and ws_stress_pd are defined elsewhere
    ws_market_losses = ws_trading_assets * ws_rate_shock / 100  # Assuming ws_trading_assets and ws_rate_shock are defined elsewhere
    ws_stress_losses = ws_credit_losses + ws_market_losses
    ws_stressed_capital = ws_total_capital - ws_stress_losses  # Assuming ws_total_capital is defined elsewhere
    ws_stressed_ratio = (ws_stressed_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100  # Assuming ws_risk_weighted_assets is defined elsewhere
    if ws_stressed_ratio >= ws_min_capital_ratio:  # Assuming ws_min_capital_ratio is defined elsewhere
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'PASS'
    else:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'FAIL'

def remediation_actions_C14() -> None:
    """Take remediation actions after a failed stress test."""
    ws_notif_type = 'stress_failure'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'URGENT: Stress test failure - action required'
    send_notification_C14()

def general_ledger_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    post_journal_entry_C14()
    balance_gl_C14()
    close_period_C14()
    generate_trial_balance_C14()

def post_journal_entry_C14() -> None:
    """Post a journal entry."""
    validate_journal_entry_C14()
    if ws_je_valid == 'Y':  # Assuming ws_je_valid is defined elsewhere
        post_to_accounts_C14()
        record_posting_C14()

def validate_journal_entry_C14() -> None:
    """Validate a journal entry."""
    global ws_je_valid, ws_total_debits, ws_total_credits, ws_je_error
    ws_je_valid = 'Y'
    ws_total_debits = 0
    ws_total_credits = 0
    for ws_je_idx in range(1, 51):
        ws_total_debits += je_debit[ws_je_idx]  # Assuming je_debit is a list/array defined elsewhere
        ws_total_credits += je_credit[ws_je_idx]  # Assuming je_credit is a list/array defined elsewhere
    if ws_total_debits != ws_total_credits:
        ws_je_valid = 'N'
        ws_je_error = 'OUT OF BALANCE'

def post_to_accounts_C14() -> None:
    """Post journal entry amounts to the GL accounts."""
    for ws_je_idx in range(1, 51):
        if je_gl_account[ws_je_idx] != ' ':  # Assuming je_gl_account is a list/array defined elsewhere
            ws_gl_account = je_gl_account[ws_je_idx]
            # Assuming read_gl_master_file and gl_master_file read a record into ws_gl_record
            ws_gl_debit_balance += je_debit[ws_je_idx]
            ws_gl_credit_balance += je_credit[ws_je_idx]
            ws_gl_net_balance = ws_gl_debit_balance - ws_gl_credit_balance
            # Assuming rewrite_gl_record rewrites the record with data from ws_gl_record

def record_posting_C14() -> None:
    """Record the journal entry posting."""
    ws_je_status = 'POSTED'
    ws_je_post_date = " " # Placeholder for current_date function
    journal_record = ws_journal_entry

def balance_gl_C14() -> None:
    """Balance the general ledger."""
    global ws_total_assets, ws_total_liabilities, ws_total_equity, ws_eof_flag, ws_balance_check, ws_error_msg
    ws_total_assets = 0
    ws_total_liabilities = 0
    ws_total_equity = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming read_gl_master_file and gl_master_file read a record into ws_gl_record
        if True: # Simulating AT END condition
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if gl_asset:  # Assuming gl_asset, gl_liability, and gl_equity are boolean variables
                ws_total_assets += ws_gl_net_balance
            elif gl_liability:
                ws_total_liabilities += ws_gl_net_balance
            elif gl_equity:
                ws_total_equity += ws_gl_net_balance

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_balance_check = ws_total_assets - ws_total_liabilities - ws_total_equity
    if ws_balance_check != 0:
        ws_error_msg = 'GL OUT OF BALANCE'
        handle_error_C14()

def close_period_C14() -> None:
    """Close the accounting period."""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':  # Assuming ws_end_of_month is defined elsewhere
        close_revenue_expense_C14()
        update_retained_earnings_C14()
        record_close_C14()

def close_revenue_expense_C14() -> None:
    """Close revenue and expense accounts."""
    global ws_net_income, ws_eof_flag
    ws_net_income = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming read_gl_master_file and gl_master_file read a record into ws_gl_record
        if True: # Simulating AT END condition
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if gl_revenue:  # Assuming gl_revenue and gl_expense are boolean variables
                ws_net_income += ws_gl_net_balance
                ws_gl_debit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_credit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_net_balance = 0
                # Assuming rewrite_gl_record rewrites the record with data from ws_gl_record
            if gl_expense:
                ws_net_income -= ws_gl_net_balance
                ws_gl_debit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_credit_balance = 0
                ws_gl_net_balance = 0
                # Assuming rewrite_gl_record rewrites the record with data from ws_gl_record

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def update_retained_earnings_C14() -> None:
    """Update the retained earnings account."""
    ws_gl_account = ws_retained_earnings_acct  # Assuming ws_retained_earnings_acct is defined elsewhere
    # Assuming read_gl_master_file and gl_master_file read a record into ws_gl_record
    ws_gl_credit_balance += ws_net_income
    ws_gl_net_balance = ws_gl_credit_balance - ws_gl_debit_balance
    # Assuming rewrite_gl_record rewrites the record with data from ws_gl_record

def record_close_C14() -> None:
    """Record the period close information."""
    ws_period_close_rec = {}  # Assuming ws_period_close_rec is a dictionary/object
    close_date = ws_process_date  # Assuming ws_process_date is defined elsewhere
    close_net_income = ws_net_income  # Assuming ws_net_income is defined elsewhere
    close_status = 'CLOSED'
    period_close_record = ws_period_close_rec

    # Assuming open_output and trial_balance_file create and open a file

    # Assuming close closes the file

def write_tb_header_C14() -> None:
    """Write the trial balance header."""
    tb_title = 'TRIAL BALANCE'
    tb_date = ws_process_date  # Assuming ws_process_date is defined elsewhere
    trial_balance_record = ws_tb_header

def write_tb_detail_C14() -> None:
    """Write the trial balance detail lines."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_tb_total_debits, ws_tb_total_credits
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming read_gl_master_file and gl_master_file read a record into ws_gl_record
        if True: # Simulating AT END condition
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            tb_account = ws_gl_account
            tb_description = ws_gl_description
            tb_debit = ws_gl_debit_balance
            tb_credit = ws_gl_credit_balance
            trial_balance_record = ws_tb_detail
            ws_tb_total_debits += ws_gl_debit_balance
            ws_tb_total_credits += ws_gl_credit_balance

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def write_tb_totals_C14() -> None:
    """Write the trial balance totals."""

    tb_debit = ws_tb_total_debits

    trial_balance_record = ws_tb_totals

def validate_call_report_C14() -> None:
    """Validate the Call Report."""
    run_validity_checks_C14()
    run_quality_checks_C14()

def run_validity_checks_C14() -> None:
    """Run validity checks on the Call Report."""
    global ws_validity_errors
    ws_validity_errors = 0
    if rc_total_assets != rc_total_loans + rc_securities + rc_other_assets:  # Assuming rc_total_assets, rc_total_loans, rc_securities, and rc_other_assets are defined elsewhere
        ws_validity_errors += 1

def run_quality_checks_C14() -> None:
    """Run quality checks on the Call Report."""
    global ws_quality_errors
    ws_quality_errors = 0
    if rc_total_assets < ws_prior_total_assets * 0.80:  # Assuming rc_total_assets and ws_prior_total_assets are defined elsewhere
        ws_quality_errors += 1

def submit_call_report_C14() -> None:
    """Submit the Call Report."""
    global ws_report_status
    if ws_validity_errors == 0:  # Assuming ws_validity_errors is defined elsewhere
        ws_report_status = 'SUBMITTED'
    else:
        ws_report_status = 'ERRORS'

def consolidate_subsidiaries_C14() -> None:
    """Consolidate subsidiary data."""
    global ws_consolidated_assets, ws_eof_flag
    ws_consolidated_assets = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming read_subsidiary_file and subsidiary_file read a record into ws_sub_rec
        if True: # Simulating AT END condition
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_consolidated_assets += sub_total_assets  # Assuming sub_total_assets is defined elsewhere
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def eliminate_intercompany_C14() -> None:
    """Eliminate intercompany transactions."""
    global ws_consolidated_assets, ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming read_intercompany_file and intercompany_file read a record into ws_ic_rec
        if True: # Simulating AT END condition
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_consolidated_assets -= ic_amount  # Assuming ic_amount is defined elsewhere
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    ccar_sec_data = ws_securities_portfolio  # Assuming ws_securities_portfolio is defined elsewhere
    ccar_trading_data = ws_trading_book  # Assuming ws_trading_book is defined elsewhere

def generate_capital_projections_C14() -> None:
    """Generate capital projections for the CCAR report."""
    for ws_quarter in range(1, 10):
        project_quarter_capital_C14(ws_quarter)

def project_quarter_capital_C14(ws_quarter: int) -> None:
    """Project capital for a given quarter."""
    ws_projected_capital = {} # Placeholder for WS_PROJECTED_CAPITAL
    ws_projected_capital[ws_quarter] = ws_starting_capital + \
                                         ws_projected_income[ws_quarter] - \
                                         ws_projected_losses[ws_quarter] - \
                                         ws_projected_dividends[ws_quarter]

def submit_ccar_C14() -> None:
    """Submit the CCAR report."""
    ws_ccar_status = 'SUBMITTED'

def generate_ctr_C14() -> None:
    """Generate Currency Transaction Reports (CTRs)."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming read_transaction_file and transaction_file read a record into ws_trans_rec
        if True: # Simulating AT END condition
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if trans_amount > 10000:  # Assuming trans_amount is defined elsewhere
                create_ctr_record_C14()
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def create_ctr_record_C14() -> None:
    """Create a Currency Transaction Report (CTR) record."""
    ws_ctr_record = {}  # Assuming ws_ctr_record is a dictionary/object
    ctr_subject = trans_customer  # Assuming trans_customer is defined elsewhere
    ctr_amount = trans_amount  # Assuming trans_amount is defined elsewhere
    ctr_date = trans_date  # Assuming trans_date is defined elsewhere
    ctr_type = 'CASH TRANSACTION'
    ctr_record = ws_ctr_record

def generate_sar_filings_C14() -> None:
    """Generate Suspicious Activity Report (SAR) filings."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

        # Assuming read_sar_pending_file and sar_pending_file read a record into ws_sar_pending
        if True: # Simulating AT END condition
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            finalize_sar_C14()
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def finalize_sar_C14() -> None:
    """Finalize a Suspicious Activity Report (SAR)."""
    sar_status = 'FILED'
    sar_filing_date = " " # Placeholder for current_date function
    sar_record = ws_sar_pending

            # Assuming ws_stmt_array is an array defined elsewhere

def evaluate_difference_C15(ws_gl_control_bal: Decimal, ws_subledger_total: Decimal, ws_recon_diff: Decimal) -> None:
    """Evaluates the difference and logs an exception if necessary."""
    ws_recon_diff = ws_gl_control_bal - ws_subledger_total
    if ws_recon_diff != Decimal('0'):
        log_recon_exception_C15()

    except Exception:
        pass
@dataclass

    recon_exc_date: str = ""

    global ws_recon_exception_C15
    ws_recon_exception_C15 = WsReconExceptionC15()
    ws_recon_exception_C15.recon_exc_account = ws_gl_account
    ws_recon_exception_C15.recon_exc_diff = ws_recon_diff
