def intercompany_recon_C15() -> None:
    """Performs intercompany reconciliation."""
    load_ic_balances_C15()
    match_ic_pairs_C15()
    report_ic_differences_C15()

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """Loads intercompany balances from file."""
    global ws_ic_count, ws_eof_flag
    ws_ic_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_ic_balance = read_intercompany_file_C15()
            ws_ic_count += 1
            ws_ic_array[ws_ic_count] = ws_ic_balance
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """Matches intercompany balance pairs."""
    ws_ic_idx = 1
    while ws_ic_idx <= ws_ic_count:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15(ws_ic_idx)
        ws_ic_idx += 1

def find_ic_counterpart_C15(ws_ic_idx: int) -> None:
    """Finds the counterpart for a given intercompany balance."""
    global ws_ic_diff
    ws_search_from = ic_from_entity[ws_ic_idx]
    ws_search_to = ic_to_entity[ws_ic_idx]
    ws_ic_idx2 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx2 <= ws_ic_count:
        if ic_from_entity[ws_ic_idx2] == ws_search_to:
            if ic_to_entity[ws_ic_idx2] == ws_search_from:
                ws_ic_diff = ic_amount[ws_ic_idx] + ic_amount[ws_ic_idx2]
                if ws_ic_diff != Decimal('0'):
                    log_ic_diff_C15(ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_diff)
                break
        ws_ic_idx2 += 1

    """Archives audit logs at the end of the month."""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        move_to_archive_C15()
        compress_archive_C15()

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """Moves audit logs older than the archive date to the archive file."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_audit_record = read_audit_file_C15()
            if ws_audit_record.ws_audit_timestamp < ws_archive_date:
                write_archive_audit_record_C15(ws_audit_record)
                delete_audit_file_C15()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    """Compresses the audit archive."""
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

    if ws_io_wait_time > ws_io_threshold:

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Calculates transaction metrics."""
    global ws_tps, ws_avg_response
    ws_tps = ws_trans_count / ws_elapsed_seconds
    ws_avg_response = ws_total_response_time / ws_trans_count

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """Analyzes performance metrics."""
    global ws_perf_degraded, ws_throughput_low
    if ws_avg_response > ws_response_threshold:
        ws_perf_degraded = 'Y'
    if ws_tps < ws_min_tps_threshold:
        ws_throughput_low = 'Y'

    """Rotates the encryption key if it is too old."""
    if ws_key_age_days > 90:
        genkey_C15()
        ws_old_key = ws_encryption_key
        ws_encryption_key = ws_new_key
        reencrypt_data_C15()

def reencrypt_data_C15() -> None:
    """Re-encrypts data with the new encryption key."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_enc_record = read_encrypted_data_file_C15()
            aes256dec_C15()
            aes256enc_C15()
            rewrite_encrypted_data_record_C15(ws_enc_record)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def authenticate_user_C15() -> None:
    """Authenticates a user."""
    global ws_auth_success, ws_failed_auth_count
    ws_auth_success = 'N'
    authuser_C15()
    if ws_auth_result == 'SUCCESS':
        ws_auth_success = 'Y'
        create_session_C15()
    else:
        log_failed_auth_C15()

def create_session_C15() -> None:
    """Creates a user session."""
    global ws_session_id, ws_session_start, ws_session_expiry
    ws_session_id = random_number_C15() * 999999999999
    ws_session_start = current_date_C15()
    ws_session_expiry = int_of_date_C15(ws_session_start) + 1

def log_failed_auth_C15() -> None:
    """Logs a failed authentication attempt."""
    global ws_failed_auth_count
    ws_failed_auth_count += 1
    if ws_failed_auth_count >= 3:
        lock_account_C15()

def lock_account_C15() -> None:
    """Locks a user account after too many failed login attempts."""
    global user_status, user_lock_date
    user_status = 'L'
    user_lock_date = current_date_C15()
    rewrite_user_record_C15()

def authorize_action_C15() -> None:
    """Authorizes a user action."""
    global ws_authorized
    ws_authorized = 'N'
    role_search_key = ws_user_role
    ws_role_perm = read_role_permission_file_C15(role_search_key)
    if ws_requested_action == role_permitted_action:
        ws_authorized = 'Y'

def log_access_C15() -> None:
    """Logs user access to the system."""
    global ws_access_log_rec_C15
    ws_access_log_rec_C15 = WsAccessLogRecC15()
    ws_access_log_rec_C15.access_log_user = ws_user_id
    ws_access_log_rec_C15.access_log_action = ws_requested_action
    ws_access_log_rec_C15.access_log_result = ws_authorized
    ws_access_log_rec_C15.access_log_timestamp = current_date_C15()
    write_access_log_record_C15(ws_access_log_rec_C15)

def security_monitoring_C15() -> None:
    """Monitors the system for security anomalies."""
    detect_anomalies_C15()
    scan_vulnerabilities_C15()
    report_incidents_C15()

def detect_anomalies_C15() -> None:
    """Detects security anomalies."""
    global ws_anomaly_detected, ws_anomaly_type
    if ws_login_count > ws_normal_login_threshold:
        ws_anomaly_detected = 'Y'
        ws_anomaly_type = 'EXCESSIVE LOGINS'
    if ws_trans_volume > ws_normal_trans_threshold:
        ws_anomaly_detected = 'Y'
        ws_anomaly_type = 'HIGH TRANSACTION VOLUME'

def scan_vulnerabilities_C15() -> None:
    """Scans the system for vulnerabilities."""
    vulnscan_C15()
    if ws_critical_vulns > 0:
        alert_security_team_C15()

def alert_security_team_C15() -> None:
    """Alerts the security team of critical vulnerabilities."""
    global ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject
    ws_notif_type = 'security_alert'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'CRITICAL: Vulnerability detected'
    send_notification_C15()

@dataclass
class WsIncidentRecordC15:
    """Data class for ws_incident_record."""
    incident_type: str = ""
    incident_date: str = ""
    incident_status: str = ""

ws_incident_record_C15 = WsIncidentRecordC15()

def report_incidents_C15() -> None:
    """Reports security incidents."""
    global ws_incident_record_C15
    if ws_anomaly_detected == 'Y':
        ws_incident_record_C15 = WsIncidentRecordC15()
        ws_incident_record_C15.incident_type = ws_anomaly_type
        ws_incident_record_C15.incident_date = current_date_C15()
        ws_incident_record_C15.incident_status = 'OPEN'
        write_incident_record_C15(ws_incident_record_C15)

    customer_profitability_C15()

from dataclasses import dataclass

cust_segment = ""
cust_total_deposits = 0
cust_loan_balances = 0

ws_eof_flag = 'N'
cust_has_checking = 'N'
cust_has_savings = 'N'

cust_income = 0
cust_total_deposits = 0
cust_id = ""
ws_opportunity = ""

def current_date_C15():
    """Placeholder for getting the current date."""
    return "2023-10-27"

def calculate_churn_risk_C15(ws_cust_rec: Any) -> None:
    """Calculates the churn risk score for a customer."""
    global cust_churn_risk, ws_churn_score
    ws_churn_score = 0
    if cust_balance_trend == 'DECLINING':
        ws_churn_score += 25
    if cust_trans_frequency == 'LOW':
        ws_churn_score += 20
    if cust_complaint_count > 2:
        ws_churn_score += 30
    if cust_tenure_months < 12:
        ws_churn_score += 25
    else:
        ws_churn_score += 0

    if ws_churn_score >= 75:
        cust_churn_risk = 'HIGH'
    elif ws_churn_score >= 50:
        cust_churn_risk = 'MEDIUM'
    else:
        cust_churn_risk = 'LOW'
    rewrite_customer_record_C15(ws_cust_rec)


def cross_sell_analysis_C15() -> None:
    """Analyzes cross-sell opportunities for customers."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = read_customer_file_C15()
            identify_opportunities_C15()
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def identify_opportunities_C15() -> None:
    """Identifies cross-sell opportunities for a customer."""
    global ws_opportunity
    if cust_has_checking == 'Y' and cust_has_savings == 'N':
        ws_opportunity = 'SAVINGS'
        create_lead_C15()
    if cust_has_mortgage == 'N' and cust_income > 75000:
        ws_opportunity = 'MORTGAGE'
        create_lead_C15()
    if cust_has_investment == 'N' and cust_total_deposits > 50000:
        ws_opportunity = 'INVESTMENT'
        create_lead_C15()


@dataclass
class WsLeadRecordC15:
    """Data class for ws_lead_record."""

    lead_customer: str = ""
    lead_product: str = ""
    lead_create_date: str = ""
    lead_status: str = ""


ws_lead_record_C15 = WsLeadRecordC15()


def create_lead_C15() -> None:
    """Creates a lead for a cross-sell opportunity."""
    global ws_lead_record_C15
    ws_lead_record_C15 = WsLeadRecordC15()
    ws_lead_record_C15.lead_customer = cust_id
    ws_lead_record_C15.lead_product = ws_opportunity
    ws_lead_record_C15.lead_create_date = current_date_C15()
    ws_lead_record_C15.lead_status = 'NEW'
    write_lead_record_C15(ws_lead_record_C15)


def retention_analysis_C15() -> None:
    """Analyzes customer retention risks."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = read_customer_file_C15()
            calculate_churn_risk_C15(ws_cust_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def calculate_segment_C15(ws_cust_rec: Any) -> None:
    """Calculates the customer segment based on relationship value."""
    global cust_segment
    ws_relationship_value = (
        cust_total_deposits + cust_loan_balances + cust_investment_value
    )
    if ws_relationship_value >= 1000000:
        cust_segment = 'private_bank'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= 250000:
        cust_segment = 'wealth_mgmt'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= 100000:
        cust_segment = 'PREFERRED'
    elif ws_relationship_value >= 25000:
        cust_segment = 'CORE'
    else:
        cust_segment = 'BASIC'
    rewrite_customer_record_C15(ws_cust_rec)
